# 人工金标 · 答题卡（原文＋翻译＋解释）

> 共 300 条｜生成器 `make_gold_set_notes.py`｜**本文件不含任何判定**

> **怎么填**：在每条末尾那一行（以「判定（决策方填）」开头）写一个数字 ——
> **1 = 是**（在说耳机/耳塞/头戴的音质）｜**0 = 不是**｜**2 = 无法判断**（产品不确定，不计入一致率）。
> **填一部分也可以**；填完群发「答题卡已交」，执行方接收（重跑本生成器会**保留**已填判定）。

> ⚠️ **锚定风险披露（执行方自查）**：下面的「解释」栏含 **声音表述** 一项，而你的判据正是「是否在说音质」——
> 该栏与判据**高度相关**，客观上会把答案偏向你。缓解：① 解析由脚本强制中性（禁判定词，命中即打回）；
> ② 提供**只看原文**的重判卡 `docs/gold_set/bias_probe_sheet.md`（40 条随机子集、可复现），
> 用 `python score_bias_probe.py` 量化「我的材料把你的判读推动了多少、往哪个方向推」。

## S2-037　G001

**原文**：I have a GeForce 750Ti and I broke the HDMI output on it.  I bought this because I found that my video card can provide audio via the DVI output.  This is a good product.  There is one issue that I find when playing video.  There is a hangup/buzzing that occurs periodically and in close succession.  It isn't something most folks would put up with.  I cannot say it is caused by the cable.  It may be my card.  I obviously did something to make the HDMI port stop working and it is reasonable to question if that impacted the card overall.

**翻译**：我有一块GeForce 750Ti，我把它的HDMI输出弄坏了。我买这个是因为我发现我的显卡可以通过DVI输出提供音频。这是个好产品。我在播放视频时发现一个问题。会周期性地、且接连不断地出现卡顿/嗡嗡声。这不是大多数人能忍受的。我不能说这是线缆造成的。可能是我的显卡。我显然做了什么事让HDMI端口停止工作，质疑这是否整体上影响了显卡是合理的。

**解释**：产品：DVI转HDMI线缆｜声音：提到播放视频时周期性地、接连不断地出现卡顿/嗡嗡声（"hangup/buzzing that occurs periodically and in close succession"），并说明不能确定是线缆造成（"I cannot say it is caused by the cable"），可能是显卡（"It may be my card"）。｜其他：提到GeForce 750Ti显卡、HDMI输出损坏、显卡可通过DVI输出提供音频、认为产品好、质疑损坏HDMI端口是否影响显卡整体。

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S1-020　G007

**原文**：I purchased the RCA ANT1450BM Refurbished Multi-Directional Flat Amplified antenna to give antenna digital feeds a try.  I wanted to try the refurbished antenna due to it's reduced price, as I have purchased refurbished tool regularly and have yet to get burned.  The price savings is like a little gamble; will it pay off? It has again with this little inexpensive antenna.  This review is as much a review of the refurbished part as much as the antenna.  This antenna is pulling in feeds from 35 miles east and 65 miles west of my location. I do live on a little elevated area for my location so I am guessing it is working in favor of my reception of these signals. I do not know what refurbishment this unit could have received, as unlike a drill or a power saw, it just either works or not, I guessing as there appears no getting into it and fixing anything like a tool.  I am happy with the results of this purchase and would recommend this antenna to others or friends.

**翻译**：我购买了RCA ANT1450BM翻新多向平板放大天线，想试试天线数字信号接收。由于价格降低，我想尝试这款翻新天线，因为我经常购买翻新工具，至今还没被坑过。省下的钱就像一场小赌博；这次会划算吗？这款便宜的小天线再次证明了划算。这篇评论既是对翻新部分的评论，也是对天线的评论。这款天线能接收我所在位置以东35英里和以西65英里的信号。我住的地方地势稍高，所以我猜这对接收这些信号有帮助。我不知道这台设备可能经过了什么翻新，因为不像钻机或电锯，它要么能用要么不能用，我猜似乎没法像工具那样打开修理。我对这次购买的结果很满意，会向其他人或朋友推荐这款天线。

**解释**：产品：RCA ANT1450BM 翻新多向平板放大天线｜声音：未提及声音｜其他：价格、翻新购买、接收距离、地理位置、推荐

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-095　G013

**原文**：My aunt and uncle have a little property where we have a large garden, work shed, and pastures.  They are both retired and wanted a way to "holler" for each other without carrying around their cell phones, especially my uncle, who works with large power tools or the animals in the pasture.  I gave them these walkie talkies, which are a great, nice and small option for out and about.  The only real drawback is that they are not the clearest.  We have been using them, and it can be a bit muffled.  You really wouldn't want to carry on a conversation with these, but they are good for quick messages.  For what we wanted them for, they are working just fine.

**翻译**：我的姑姑和姑父有一小块地，我们在那里有一个大花园、工具棚和牧场。他们俩都退休了，想要一种不用随身携带手机就能互相“喊话”的方式，尤其是我的姑父，他要操作大型电动工具，或者在牧场里照看牲畜。我把这些对讲机给了他们，对于外出走动来说，它们是个很棒、小巧的好选择。唯一真正的缺点是它们不够清晰。我们一直在用它们，声音会有点闷。你真的不会想用这些来进行对话，但它们很适合快速传递消息。就我们想要它们派上的用场而言，它们用起来完全没问题。

**解释**：产品：对讲机｜声音：不够清晰（not the clearest），声音会有点闷（a bit muffled），不适合进行对话（wouldn't want to carry on a conversation），适合快速传递消息（good for quick messages）｜其他：姑姑和姑父有一小块地，有花园、工具棚和牧场；两人都已退休；想要不用随身携带手机就能互相喊话的方式；姑父要操作大型电动工具或照看牧场里的牲畜；对讲机小巧，适合外出走动；就他们的用途而言用起来没问题

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-068　G014

**原文**：I recently started working from home and have been using my AirPod pros to make and take calls. Using my AirPods 8-10 hours a day has really irritated my ear drums. Came across this headset and thought to give it a try. I really like the comfort feel of the headset versus sticking my AirPods in my ear. Now don’t get me wrong the AirPods are great for my runs and going to the gym, but having a headset separate for work and not irritating my ear drums is a MUST. My clients can hear me clear and there’s no static. Truly a great item to consider

**翻译**：我最近开始在家工作，一直用我的AirPods Pro拨打和接听电话。每天使用AirPods 8-10小时真的刺激了我的耳膜。偶然看到这款耳机，想试一试。相比把AirPods塞进耳朵里，我真的很喜欢这款耳机的舒适感。别误会，AirPods对于我跑步和去健身房来说很棒，但有一个单独用于工作且不刺激耳膜的耳机是必须的。我的客户能清楚地听到我，而且没有静电噪音。真的是一件值得考虑的好物品。

**解释**：产品：耳机｜声音：客户能清楚地听到我，而且没有静电噪音（"My clients can hear me clear and there’s no static"）｜其他：在家工作，每天使用AirPods 8-10小时刺激耳膜，喜欢这款耳机的舒适感，AirPods适合跑步和健身房，需要单独的工作耳机

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-026　G015

**原文**：I'm a smaller woman, and found the grip on my Canon 30D to be uncomfortable, but I wasn't willing to compromise on photo quality so a digicam was out.  When I discovered micro four thirds cameras, I tried out the Oly E-P1 and E-LP1, but preferred the feel of the GF1.  I also tried out the Samsung NX10, but it just doesn't offer enough native lenses for me.<br /><br />The GF1 is the most comfortable camera I've ever owned.  The image quality is very good, and although I hear it's good on full auto, I use mine on full manual or shutter priority, and it's very easy to use if you've had any experience with a full featured camera. The buttons and menus are very intuitive, the wheel at the top of the camera is easy to use and I like the flash to use as a fill flash.  At default I got a few blown highlights, but this is very easy to control by stepping down just a little.<br /><br />The only things that I didn't love are the rear dial - it's a little more recessed than I'd like, although I got used to it pretty quickly - and I would have preferred a flip and twist screen since the camera doesn't come with a view finder.  Otherwise I'm very satisfied with this camera.<br /><br />The 14-45mm kit lens is good quality.  It's solid with no rattles, turns with just enough resistance, and takes sharp high quality pics with very little chromatic aberration.<br /><br />One thing last worth mentioning is the stabilization.  It's in the lens, and it works better than I expected.  I got handheld pics reliably at speeds I haven't been able to shoot at before.  That being said, if you prefer your stabilization in the camera rather than in the lens, the E-PL1 is definitely worth checking out.<br /><br />I'm very happy with the GF1, particularly the easy menus and controls.  It takes the annoyances out of photography so I can concentrate on taking the pics rather than fighting the camera.  With good image quality, nice videos, fast auto focus and better than average auto features, this seems like a camera for anyone interested photography, from newbie to pros looking for a smaller second camera.  For enthusiasts like me, it's just the ticket!

**翻译**：我是一位身材较小的女性，发现我的佳能30D的握持感不舒服，但我不愿意在照片质量上妥协，所以卡片机出局了。当我发现微四三相机时，我试用了奥林巴斯E-P1和E-PL1，但更喜欢GF1的手感。我也试用了三星NX10，但它的原生镜头对我来说不够多。<br /><br />GF1是我拥有过的最舒适的相机。图像质量非常好，虽然我听说全自动模式很好，但我用全手动或快门优先，如果你有任何全功能相机的经验，它非常容易使用。按钮和菜单非常直观，相机顶部的拨轮很容易使用，我喜欢用闪光灯作为补光。在默认设置下，我得到了一些过曝的高光，但通过稍微调低一点很容易控制。<br /><br />唯一我不太喜欢的是后拨盘——它比我想要的更凹进去一些，尽管我很快就习惯了——而且我希望有翻转和旋转屏幕，因为相机没有取景器。除此之外，我对这台相机非常满意。<br /><br />14-45mm套机镜头质量很好。它很坚固，没有松动，转动时有恰到好处的阻力，拍摄出清晰的高质量照片，色差非常小。<br /><br />最后值得一提的一件事是防抖。它在镜头里，效果比我预期的要好。我能在以前无法拍摄的速度下可靠地手持拍摄。话虽如此，如果你更喜欢相机内防抖而不是镜头防抖，E-PL1绝对值得一看。<br /><br />我对GF1非常满意，特别是简单的菜单和控制。它消除了摄影中的烦恼，让我可以专注于拍照而不是和相机较劲。凭借良好的图像质量、不错的视频、快速自动对焦和高于平均的自动功能，这似乎是一款适合任何对摄影感兴趣的人的相机，从新手到寻找更小备用相机的专业人士。对于像我这样的爱好者来说，它正合适！

**解释**：产品：微四三相机（松下GF1）｜声音：未提及声音｜其他：握持感、图像质量、按钮和菜单、拨轮、闪光灯、高光控制、后拨盘、屏幕、套机镜头、防抖、自动对焦、视频

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S3-011　G020

**原文**：One of the few headsets that has no volume control.  This alone disqualifies the headset.  The mic quality is actually decent and as a spare headset for travel, this may be an OK option - but not a default option.  There is no active noise cancellation (obviously), may block some noise - but on an airplane system, this didnt give enough volume or noise blocking to be useful in airplane entertainment systems either.  Not worth taking a chance on this model.

**翻译**：这是少数没有音量控制的耳机之一。仅此一点就使这款耳机不合格。麦克风质量实际上还不错，作为旅行备用耳机，这可能是一个可以的选择——但不是默认选择。没有主动降噪（显然），可能会阻挡一些噪音——但在飞机系统上，这既没有提供足够的音量，也没有足够的噪音阻挡，因此在飞机娱乐系统中也不实用。不值得冒险尝试这个型号。

**解释**：产品：耳机｜声音：提到没有音量控制（no volume control），没有主动降噪（no active noise cancellation），可能阻挡一些噪音（may block some noise），在飞机系统上音量不足且噪音阻挡不够（didnt give enough volume or noise blocking）｜其他：麦克风质量（mic quality is actually decent），作为旅行备用耳机（spare headset for travel），不值得冒险尝试（Not worth taking a chance）

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

This cable fits comfortably in the USB C slot and is not wobbly. You get better video and audio than with the normal hdmi to hdmi.

**解释**：产品：USB C转HDMI线｜声音：表示相比普通HDMI转HDMI能获得更好的音频（better video and audio）｜其他：提到USB C在手机、笔记本电脑、台式机中的广泛使用；线缆插入USB C插槽舒适且不晃动（fits comfortably, not wobbly）；便于连接电视、显示器或投影仪

## S3-045　G022

**原文**：Very very disappointed. I own a 350xt from the same manufacturer and it works great.<br />This &#34;point&#34; product does not have clear sound, it feels you are far away from the speaker...and then for the people you talk to, they do not hear you well. ## S3-055　G025

**原文**：Note: I would love to love this speaker set, but it has inconsistent performance with various devices. I received a review sample and was pleased by the sound when I plugged this into the phone port on my ThinkPad T61. The sound was clear and I could hear every note on the album that I use to test speakers (Miles Davis' [[ASIN:B000002ADT Kind of Blue]]). I use that album because the instruments in the ensemble cover low, mid and high range frequencies. The bass was a little thin, but with the speakers less than two feet away the sound was rich and I was happy.<br /><br />Where I started to experience problems was when I plugged this into the 3.5mm port on my Samsung Galaxy Note 3 phone. There was static in the mix. The same with my [[ASIN:B00D029NNA Samsung Galaxy Tab 3 10.1 inch]] - static interfered with the music. My final test was to plug the 3.5mm cable end into an [[ASIN:B00AEJJRBK Avantree Cara Bluetooth 4.0 Handsfree stereo Car Kit with mic]] and connect via bluetooth. The sound was crystal clear.<br /><br />I am not sure what conclusions to draw. It could be something wrong with the Samsung phone and tablet, although both work perfectly with headphones which I have directly plugged into their 3.5mm ports. It may be that the 3.5mm cable and interface on the input of this speaker is overly sensitive to impedance and the slightest deviation will induce static. I am not sure, but because I was unable to get the same quality consistently across all devices with which I tested these speakers I subtracted two stars.<br /><br />Construction is exceptional, although I wish the design employed removable USB and 3.5mm cables instead of the fixed ones that provide power and signal inputs, and connect the two units. As it stands if a cable breaks it will take some skill to repair it. With removable, off-the-shelf cables a broken one can be rectified with a trip to an electronics store.<br /><br />Overall, on the two devices that I tested with which these speakers worked the sound was spectacular. On the other two it was unlistenable. If you are connecting this to a bluetooth receiver it's probably a safe choice, but if you are connecting this to anything else I would proceed with caution.

**翻译**：注意：我很想喜欢这套音箱，但它与不同设备的性能表现不一致。我收到了一个评测样品，当我把它插到我的ThinkPad T61的电话端口时，我对音质感到满意。声音清晰，我能听到我用来测试音箱的专辑（Miles Davis的[[ASIN:B000002ADT Kind of Blue]]）中的每一个音符。我用那张专辑是因为合奏中的乐器覆盖了低频、中频和高频范围。低音有点单薄，但音箱距离不到两英尺时，声音丰富，我很高兴。<br /><br />我开始遇到问题是在我把它插到我的三星Galaxy Note 3手机的3.5毫米端口时。混音中有静电噪声。我的[[ASIN:B00D029NNA 三星Galaxy Tab 3 10.1英寸]]也是如此——静电干扰了音乐。我的最后一项测试是将3.5毫米线缆端插入[[ASIN:B00AEJJRBK Avantree Cara蓝牙4.0免提立体声车载套件（带麦克风）]]并通过蓝牙连接。声音非常清晰。<br /><br />我不确定该得出什么结论。可能是三星手机和平板有问题，尽管两者与我直接插入其3.5毫米端口的耳机配合完美。可能是这个音箱输入的3.5毫米线缆和接口对阻抗过于敏感，任何轻微偏差都会引起静电。我不确定，但由于我无法在所有测试设备上获得一致的质量，我扣了两颗星。<br /><br />做工非常出色，尽管我希望设计采用可拆卸的USB和3.5毫米线缆，而不是固定的提供电源和信号输入并连接两个单元的线缆。目前如果线缆断裂，修复需要一些技巧。使用可拆卸的现成线缆，损坏的线缆可以通过去电子商店一趟来解决。<br /><br />总体而言，在我测试的与这些音箱配合工作的两个设备上，声音非常出色。在另外两个设备上则无法聆听。如果你将其连接到蓝牙接收器，可能是一个安全的选择，但如果你将其连接到其他任何设备，我会谨慎行事。

**解释**：产品：音箱｜声音：在ThinkPad T61上声音清晰，能听到每个音符，低音有点单薄但近距离声音丰富；在三星Galaxy Note 3和Galaxy Tab 3上有静电干扰；通过蓝牙连接Avantree Cara时声音非常清晰；在配合工作的设备上声音非常出色，在另外两个设备上无法聆听。｜其他：与不同设备的性能表现不一致；做工非常出色；希望采用可拆卸的USB和3.5毫米线缆而非固定线缆；扣了两颗星；建议连接蓝牙接收器可能安全，连接其他设备需谨慎。

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S1-048　G029

**原文**：I ordered the beats and was ready to send them back because they kept falling out my ears. My beats are now secure in my ear and I can do almost anything without worrying about them falling out! The best part about it is the variety of colors that I have! I would definitely recommend these, worth the money!

## S3-089　G032

**原文**：I bought this NAS to replace a desktop PC that I had loaded with drives and used as a server.  It put out too much heat for my new office, so I wanted something more efficient.  After some research, this is the NAS I selected and I have no regrets.<br /><br />The NAS is very quiet and low heat.  The drives 'sleep' when not in use and performance in accessing drives is much better than the 'server' it replaced.  I am very impressed.<br /><br />Set up was easy, using 2x1TB drives from the old PC.  Install drives, format drives (all data lost), update firmware, set up functions and your off to the races.  I use an external USB attached drive to back up selected contents of the NAS for 'belt and suspenders' backup.<br /><br />There are quite a number of functions the NAS is capable of that I simply don't exploit.  If you're looking for a media server, etc, check out the functions that this device provides in a space not much larger than a pair of 3.5&#34; drives.<br /><br />I think this device provides a great value.  Just remember that it's diskless - so factor in the cost of 2 drives to your setup.<br /><br />Steve

**翻译**：我买这个NAS是为了替换一台我装满了硬盘并用作服务器的台式电脑。它给我的新办公室带来了太多热量，所以我想要更高效的东西。经过一番研究，这就是我选择的NAS，我没有任何遗憾。<br /><br />这个NAS非常安静，热量低。硬盘在不使用时‘休眠’，访问硬盘的性能比它替换的‘服务器’好得多。我非常印象深刻。<br /><br />设置很容易，使用旧电脑上的2块1TB硬盘。安装硬盘，格式化硬盘（所有数据丢失），更新固件，设置功能，然后你就可以开始了。我使用一个外接USB硬盘来备份NAS的选定内容，作为‘双保险’备份。<br /><br />这个NAS有很多功能我根本没有利用。如果你在寻找媒体服务器等，看看这个设备提供的功能，它的大小不超过一对3.5英寸硬盘。<br /><br />我认为这个设备提供了很大的价值。只要记住它是无盘的——所以要把2块硬盘的成本算进你的设置中。<br /><br />Steve

**解释**：产品：NAS（网络附加存储）｜声音：非常安静（very quiet），硬盘在不使用时‘休眠’（drives 'sleep' when not in use）｜其他：低热量（low heat），性能比替换的服务器好（performance in accessing drives is much better than the 'server' it replaced），设置容易（Set up was easy），使用2块1TB硬盘，格式化硬盘（format drives），更新固件（update firmware），设置功能（set up functions），外接USB硬盘备份（external USB attached drive to back up），许多功能未利用（quite a number of functions... don't exploit），媒体服务器（media server），大小不超过一对3.5英寸硬盘（space not much larger than a pair of 3.5" drives），提供很大价值（provides a great value），无盘（diskless），考虑2块硬盘的成本（factor in the cost of 2 drives）

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-013　G035

**原文**：Quality of build is evident in the Canon SX260 HS. Buttons and levers appear to be solid in both function and design; audible shutter activation is crisp and professional. The ability to set the F Stop, Shutter Speed and ISO manually, other than using the Auto setting is what makes this camera very special. The CMOS sensor adds to the total package, delivering crisp, professional photos. Images taken with the long zoom lens, despite it's 20X capabilities and being hand-held, were devoid of any distortion and held a high degree of resolution.<br /><br />Cons: Battery life appeared notably short, perhaps due to using the zoom and GPS feature often. This has yet to be confirmed.<br />  The location of the pop-up flash is where one would normally place an index finger over the top of the camera.<br />  A conscious effort must be made to not interfere with the flash mechanism when using this camera.

**翻译**：佳能 SX260 HS 的做工质量显而易见。按钮和拨杆在功能和设计上都显得很扎实；可听见的快门启动声清脆而专业。除了使用自动设置外，还能手动设置光圈、快门速度和 ISO，这正是这款相机非常特别之处。CMOS 传感器为整体加分，带来清晰、专业的照片。尽管长焦镜头具有 20 倍能力且为手持拍摄，所拍图像没有任何畸变，并保持了高分辨率。<br /><br />缺点：电池续航似乎明显偏短，也许是因为经常使用变焦和 GPS 功能。这一点尚未得到证实。<br />  弹出式闪光灯的位置正好是人们通常将食指放在相机顶部的地方。<br />  使用这款相机时，必须有意识地避免干扰闪光灯机构。

**解释**：产品：佳能 SX260 HS 数码相机｜声音：可听见的快门启动声清脆而专业（audible shutter activation is crisp and professional）｜其他：做工质量、按钮和拨杆、手动设置光圈/快门速度/ISO、CMOS 传感器、长焦镜头 20 倍变焦、手持拍摄、图像无畸变、高分辨率、电池续航短、GPS 功能、弹出式闪光灯位置、避免干扰闪光灯机构

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-084　G040

**原文**：There is a lot to like with this computer: The small size is great. There are 2 USB-C ports instead of just 1 like with a lot of others. There is no bloatware. You can hook up your monitor to either the DisplayPort or USB-C connection. They include a USB-C to HDMI adapter. There are 4 USB 3.0 ports. Good speed for regular computer use with the I7 processor and SSD.<br /><br />The biggest thing to dislike is the volume of the fan. You wouldn't think a computer this small would have such a loud fan, but it does. It's annoyingly loud. If you're going to be working on the computer a lot while in a silent room, I would recommend to not buy this computer because of the fan noise.

**翻译**：这台电脑有很多值得喜欢的地方：体积小很棒。有2个USB-C端口，而不是像很多其他电脑那样只有1个。没有预装垃圾软件。你可以将显示器连接到DisplayPort或USB-C接口。他们附带了USB-C转HDMI适配器。有4个USB 3.0端口。凭借I7处理器和固态硬盘，日常电脑使用速度良好。<br /><br />最不喜欢的是风扇的噪音。你不会想到这么小的电脑会有这么响的风扇，但它确实有。它响得烦人。如果你要在安静的房间里长时间使用电脑，我会建议不要买这台电脑，因为风扇噪音。

**解释**：产品：电脑｜声音：风扇噪音大（'the volume of the fan'、'such a loud fan'、'annoyingly loud'、'fan noise'）｜其他：体积小、2个USB-C端口、无预装软件、DisplayPort或USB-C连接显示器、附带USB-C转HDMI适配器、4个USB 3.0端口、I7处理器和固态硬盘速度良好

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-078　G041

**原文**：Smart watches have gotten more popular with the release of the Apple watch. They tend to have a standard set of functions, such as access to instant messaging, email, time, alarms and a lot more, typically tied to your phone. Not all smart watches are alike, though, and offer different sets of functions, better connectivity and other various differences from one another. This particular smart watch offers the mainline set of functions, setting itself apart through the user interface which is more polished and clean than most others with which I have worked. That said, I have a couple of quibbles with it, leaving room for a little improvement.<br /><br />This is a standard-sized watch with a rubber wristband that can actually fit a larger wrist like mine. I have trouble finding watches and watch bands that fit me, so the fact that this one fits me is a pleasant surprise.<br /><br />Powering it on, the user is immediately greeted with a complex and fairly complete visual menu system with tiles for every function available to the user. This is a standard presentation for a smartwatch, but this one looks a bit more polished and clean than the others I have seen. Switching to other menu screens just requires a swipe gesture, as to be expected. I have big hands, and this means that, for me, using a stylus makes working with this watch a lot easier. This is not the fault of the watch but a reality of working with a small device.<br /><br />My first issue with this watch came when I paired it to my phone. The date and time on the watch were off, so I expected that it would grab the time from my iPhone when I paired it. It did not. Instead, I had to dig for the clock application and manually set the time. I dislike this and think that having it set itself when it is paired is much more accurate and cuts effort on the part of the user. For Android users, there is an application to allow for syncing up the time, date and more, but iOS users are out of luck for now. To the manufacturer's credit this is made clear on the product listing so this is not a surprise. Regardless, I am of the opinion that the watch should fetch the time, not rely on being sent the time. In the grand scheme of things, though, this is an annoyance, not a deal breaker.<br /><br />There is a fairly comprehensive list of functions provided by this watch in addition to time/date and phone answer. Through the menus the user has access to call logs and function, Bluetooth functions, a remote finder function, a pedometer, a sleep monitor, a reminder function to get up and move on occasion, calendar and clock functions, audio functions, video functions and a few more.<br /><br />One of the interesting functions that this watch offers is a camera. So, I tried to use it and it complained that there was no memory card available. Reading through the limited documentation included, I was unable to find anything about a memory card. I finally noticed that the back of the watch has a small groove where it can be popped off, and when I removed the back I found the battery which is removable (replaceable - another pleasant surprise). Under the battery is a slot for a micro SD card.<br /><br />Adding a memory card comes with a big catch. It requires removing the battery to get at the holder which in turn clears the clock and resets it to midnight 01-01-2015. So, adding the memory card requires me to set the time again. And again if I remove the card.<br /><br />Although I think the time sync functions of this watch are lacking, it is otherwise a good smartwatch. The interface is professional and polished and the whole provides access to a large number of useful functions. It's priced well too. Overall, this is a solid offering worth a look if you are wanting a smartwatch.<br /><br />Disclosure: I was provided a sample of this product in exchange for a fair, honest and thorough review. Even though I received this product at no/minimal cost I have rated and reviewed this product without bias, based solely on my experience with it.

**翻译**：随着Apple Watch的发布，智能手表变得越来越流行。它们通常有一套标准功能，比如访问即时通讯、电子邮件、时间、闹钟等等，通常与你的手机相连。不过，并非所有智能手表都一样，它们提供不同的功能组合、更好的连接性以及彼此之间的其他各种差异。这款智能手表提供了主流的功能组合，通过比大多数我用过的其他手表更精致、更简洁的用户界面而脱颖而出。话虽如此，我对它有几个小意见，还有一点改进的空间。<br /><br />这是一款标准尺寸的手表，配有橡胶腕带，实际上能适合像我这样较大的手腕。我很难找到适合我的手表和表带，所以这款能适合我，是个令人愉快的惊喜。<br /><br />开机后，用户会立即看到一个复杂且相当完整的视觉菜单系统，每个可用功能都有对应的磁贴。这是智能手表的标准呈现方式，但这款看起来比我见过的其他手表更精致、更简洁。切换到其他菜单屏幕只需滑动手势，正如预期的那样。我的手很大，这意味着对我来说，使用触控笔操作这款手表要容易得多。这不是手表的问题，而是操作小型设备的现实。<br /><br />我对这款手表的第一个问题出现在将它配对到手机时。手表上的日期和时间不对，所以我以为配对时它会从我的iPhone获取时间。但它没有。相反，我不得不翻找时钟应用程序并手动设置时间。我不喜欢这一点，认为配对时自动设置会更准确，也能减少用户的麻烦。对于Android用户，有一个应用程序可以同步时间、日期等，但iOS用户目前就没那么幸运了。值得肯定的是，制造商在产品列表中已明确说明，所以这并不意外。尽管如此，我认为手表应该主动获取时间，而不是依赖被发送时间。不过，从大局来看，这只是个烦人的小问题，不是决定性的缺陷。<br /><br />除了时间/日期和接听电话外，这款手表还提供了相当全面的功能列表。通过菜单，用户可以访问通话记录和功能、蓝牙功能、遥控查找功能、计步器、睡眠监测、偶尔提醒起身活动的功能、日历和时钟功能、音频功能、视频功能等。<br /><br />这款手表提供的一个有趣功能是摄像头。于是，我尝试使用它，它却提示没有可用的存储卡。阅读随附的有限文档，我找不到任何关于存储卡的信息。最后我注意到手表背面有一个小凹槽，

**解释**：产品：智能手表｜声音：评论者未提及声音。｜其他：评论者提到：与Apple Watch相比的流行度；标准功能如即时通讯、电子邮件、时间、闹钟；用户界面精致简洁；橡胶腕带适合较大手腕；开机后视觉菜单系统；滑动手势切换菜单；大手掌使用触控笔；与iPhone配对时日期时间未自动同步，需手动设置，Android有同步应用而iOS没有；功能列表包括通话记录、蓝牙、遥控查找、计步器、睡眠监测、提醒、日历、时钟、音频、视频；摄像头功能提示无存储卡，文档未提及，发现手表背面有小凹槽。

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-055　G042

**原文**：There are things that I like and dislike about this computer. Much of it is simply personal preference over the actual computer.<br /><br />Pros:<br />- I really like that it has face detection to unlock the screen.<br />- The colors of the keyboard are really cool<br />-the picture color is amazing. I watch videos and tv shows on it often because I enjoy the clarity of the picture<br /><br />Cons:<br />-Cost<br />- smaller screen (which I know) but there is not a side number keyboard which I really prefer.<br />- It can be difficult to open.<br /><br />I don't use this computer for gaming purposes. I am a teacher and I use it for classroom at home on the weekends. I would buy this computer again... except for the cost. If it was about $900.00 cheaper. Compared to other laptops that I have, I see no difference over all in this computer and others. It just simply isn't worth the cost in my opinion. However, that is a personal reason and I will not take off from the review for that.

**翻译**：关于这台电脑，我有喜欢和不喜欢的地方。其中很多只是个人偏好，而非电脑本身的问题。<br /><br />优点：<br />- 我非常喜欢它有人脸识别解锁屏幕的功能。<br />- 键盘的颜色真的很酷。<br />- 画面色彩非常棒。我经常用它看视频和电视节目，因为我很享受画面的清晰度。<br /><br />缺点：<br />- 价格。<br />- 屏幕较小（这点我知道），但没有侧边数字键盘，而我真的很喜欢有数字键盘。<br />- 打开它可能有些困难。<br /><br />我不使用这台电脑玩游戏。我是一名教师，周末在家里的课堂上使用它。我会再次购买这台电脑……如果不是因为价格的话。如果它能便宜大约900美元的话。与我有过的其他笔记本电脑相比，我认为这台电脑和其他电脑总体上没有区别。在我看来，它根本不值这个价。然而，这是个人原因，我不会因此而在评价中扣分。

**解释**：产品：笔记本电脑｜声音：未提及｜其他：提及了人脸识别解锁、键盘颜色、画面色彩、价格、屏幕尺寸、缺少数字键盘、打开困难、使用场景（教师、周末在家上课、不玩游戏）、与其他笔记本电脑的比较、以及是否愿意再次购买。

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S3-034　G043

**原文**：I bought 6 of these when they were released with the bonus offer.  I got them for a move to a new home.  Because of the delay between receiving & setting up the units I missed the return window.  All 6 would have been shipped back for a refund.<br /><br />I have both The ECHO & TAP, love them both.  My primary very simple, not very technical use is as a  cooking & alarm timer & playing music.  The volume is far too low to hear the timer unless I'm in the kitchen.  My other units would blast me out of the house if I had the timer set on volume 10.  The DOT can not be heard in the next room with a simple wall & full door opening.  The microwave is louder.  I can't count on it as an alarm, reminder to take my meds or anything else.<br /><br />The quality of the sound for music is like an old AM radio.  Not worth listening to.  Yes, I know I can go buy extra speakers but it defeats the purpose of buying a small compact unit.  I am actually going to be giving them away & will give GOOGLE Home a try.  That's how disappointed I am in this AMAZON product.  AND, I love AMAZON so this doesn't sit well

**翻译**：我在这些产品发布时购买了6个，当时有赠品优惠。我买它们是为了搬到新家。由于收到和设置之间的延迟，我错过了退货窗口。所有6个都会退回以获得退款。<br /><br />我同时拥有ECHO和TAP，都很喜欢。我主要非常简单的、不太技术性的用途是作为烹饪和闹钟计时器以及播放音乐。音量太低，除非我在厨房，否则听不到计时器。我的其他设备如果我把计时器音量设为10，会把我震出房子。DOT在隔壁房间，即使只有一堵墙和完全打开的门，也听不到。微波炉的声音更大。我不能依赖它作为闹钟、提醒吃药或其他任何事情。<br /><br />音乐的音质就像老式AM收音机。不值得听。是的，我知道我可以买额外的扬声器，但这违背了购买小型紧凑设备的初衷。我实际上打算把它们送人，并尝试GOOGLE Home。这就是我对这个AMAZON产品的失望程度。而且，我喜欢AMAZON，所以这让我感觉不好。

**解释**：产品：Amazon Echo Dot｜声音：音量太低，除非在厨房，否则听不到计时器（'The volume is far too low to hear the timer unless I'm in the kitchen'）；在隔壁房间，即使只有一堵墙和完全打开的门，也听不到（'The DOT can not be heard in the next room with a simple wall & full door opening'）；微波炉的声音更大（'The microwave is louder'）；音乐的音质就像老式AM收音机，不值得听（'The quality of the sound for music is like an old AM radio. Not worth listening to'）。｜其他：购买了6个，因搬家延迟设置而错过退货窗口；拥有ECHO和TAP，喜欢它们；主要用途是烹饪和闹钟计时器以及播放音乐；不能依赖它作为闹钟或提醒吃药；打算送人并尝试GOOGLE Home；喜欢AMAZON，所以感到失望。

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-011　G050

**原文**：I'm not a fan of the ultra-thin profiles of most tablets.  Understandably if its a smart phone you want it to slip into a pocket easily but with a tablet for home use there isn't much occasion where it needs to be ultra-slim and consequently unwieldy and as secure as a wet bar of soap.  That being said, I know these are meant to be travel friendly and as such I'm glad that they are small and sleek and, in the case of the Lenovo M8 HD, power misers as well.  I'm okay with this because I occasionally travel with my tablet and because the remedy for the non-tactile properties of the ultra-sleek tablet is a silicone case like this one.  They're not expensive, they do a darned good job of protecting the tablet, and they make the tablet much easier to handle in and around the house.<br /><br />Normally I don't look for a feature rich version of the silicone case.  It's usually going to be just a simple stretch into place "skin" of sorts where corners are protected and screen is recessed to where it is protected from drops and such.  Those usually hover around the $10 mark or so.  What intrigued me about this case was the rotating stand and the handstrap feature.  I use the tablet in bed often as it sits on my nightstand.  I also occasionally use it in the kitchen or bathroom too.  So those features would undoubtedly come in handy.  My first thought was that paying double the cost was a little out of form but that was followed by, "what's another $10?"<br /><br />The case installs in parts that are intuitive and easy to fit.  Some stretching is required and some massaging of corners to get everything to fit.  But once in place everything fits perfectly.  Buttons work great and have good feel, the power jack is covered by a silicone flap which is easy to access as needed.  Cameras front and back show fine and the speaker is exposed.  The case itself is nice and hefty, easy to carry, non-slip in enough areas, and dare I say "bouncy" in all the right places.<br /><br />I removed the shoulder strap retainers that came pre-installed as I don't see myself carrying the case that way.  I put those away with the shoulder strap as a just-in-case item for later.  The handstrap adjusted with velcro easily and the stand works great.  The fact that the stand/strap mount rotates 360 degrees is awesome as it adapts to whatever angle your hand is at whatever orientation that you're standing the tablet on any flat surface.  This was of instant benefit as I slipped my hand in and it felt both comfortable and secure.<br /><br />The one feature I do wish it had was covers for the cameras.  I ended up 3D printing some covers for it.  I also feel like there is little to no bezel clearance for a tempered glass screen protector although the case itself has enough flex to accommodate it anyway so I'll be adding one in the near future.  That being said the case provides a substantial amount of protection for the screen as long as it gets dropped on a flat surface.  Conclusion: Well worth the money as it is grippy, wieldy, standy, grabby, and functionally perfect.

**翻译**：我不喜欢大多数平板电脑的超薄外形。可以理解，如果是智能手机，你希望它能轻松放进口袋，但对于家用平板电脑来说，并没有太多场合需要它超薄，从而变得笨重且像湿肥皂一样不安全。话虽如此，我知道这些平板电脑是为了便于旅行而设计的，因此我很高兴它们小巧时尚，而且就联想M8 HD而言，还省电。我可以接受这一点，因为我偶尔会带着平板电脑旅行，而且对于超光滑平板电脑缺乏触感的问题，解决办法就是像这样的硅胶保护套。它们不贵，能很好地保护平板电脑，并且让平板电脑在家里和周围更容易拿握。<br /><br />通常我不会寻找功能丰富的硅胶保护套。它通常只是一个简单的拉伸到位的“皮肤”，角落得到保护，屏幕凹陷以保护其免受跌落等。这些通常价格在10美元左右。这个保护套吸引我的是旋转支架和手带功能。我经常在床上使用平板电脑，因为它放在我的床头柜上。我偶尔也会在厨房或浴室使用它。所以这些功能无疑会派上用场。我最初的想法是支付双倍价格有点不合理，但随后又想，“再多10美元又怎样？”<br /><br />保护套分部分安装，直观且易于安装。需要一些拉伸和角落的调整才能全部安装到位。但一旦安装好，一切都完美贴合。按钮工作良好，手感不错，电源插孔由硅胶盖覆盖，需要时易于打开。前后摄像头显示正常，扬声器暴露在外。保护套本身厚实，易于携带，在足够多的区域防滑，而且我敢说在所有正确的地方都“有弹性”。<br /><br />我取下了预装的肩带固定器，因为我不认为自己会那样携带保护套。我把它们和肩带一起收起来，作为以后的备用物品。手带通过魔术贴轻松调整，支架工作良好。支架/手带安装座可以360度旋转，这太棒了，因为它可以适应你手在任何角度以及你以任何方向将平板电脑立在平坦表面上的情况。这立即带来了好处，我把手伸进去，感觉既舒适又安全。<br /><br />我希望它有的一个功能是摄像头盖。我最终3D打印了一些盖子。我还觉得钢化玻璃屏幕保护膜几乎没有边框间隙，尽管保护套本身有足够的柔韧性来容纳它，所以我不久后会添加一个。话虽如此，只要掉落在平坦表面上，保护套为屏幕提供了大量的保护。结论：我们

**解释**：产品：联想M8 HD平板电脑硅胶保护套｜声音：未提及声音｜其他：讨论平板电脑超薄设计、硅胶保护套的安装、旋转支架、手带、肩带、摄像头盖、屏幕保护膜、保护性能、价格、便携性、防滑性、按钮、电源插孔、摄像头、扬声器

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

**原文**：[[VIDEOID:5ccab2044675ebd47957448b1e0ac5e8]] I think this will actually be a useful item, as opposed to just being a cute gimmick.<br /><br />The fan is all plastic, of course, but it seems to work really well. ## S1-051　G068

**原文**：This is by far the best kindle to date, for me anyway.  The battery life is terrific and I love I can place it on a charging base [I have a Amazon basic brand, I didn’t need to buy the promoted bundle].  The page turner is so much faster and I love the larger screen and the dark mode reading: less lighting up.  I traded in the 2018 kindle, which was a dud imho with the power switch so super sensitive that it wouldn’t take much of a touch to switch off the kindle..it was so annoying.  Happy now and even though the memory is more than I need for my needs, I am enjoying the reading experience like the earlier models, but now with some great new features.  Happy reader.  After few months of use, I finally traded in my 3G kindle, which was more reliable and acted as backup for the previous unreliable model.  I hope Amazon is able to recycle these older models.

**翻译**：对我来说，这是迄今为止最好的Kindle。电池续航非常棒，而且我喜欢可以把它放在充电底座上（我用的是Amazon基础品牌，不需要买推广的套装）。翻页速度快多了，我喜欢更大的屏幕和深色模式阅读：减少亮光。我折价换掉了2018年的Kindle，在我看来那是个失败品，电源开关太敏感了，轻轻一碰就会关掉Kindle，太烦人了。现在很开心，虽然内存对我来说超出了需求，但我像早期型号一样享受阅读体验，而且现在还有一些很棒的新功能。快乐的读者。用了几个月后，我终于折价换掉了我的3G Kindle，它更可靠，作为之前不可靠型号的备用。我希望亚马逊能够回收这些旧型号。

**解释**：产品：Kindle电子书阅读器｜声音：未提及声音相关内容｜其他：电池续航、充电底座、翻页速度、屏幕尺寸、深色模式、折价换旧、电源开关敏感度、内存、回收旧型号

## S3-001　G072

**原文**：First and foremost let's get to the issue of PU leather, check Google or Wikipedia, it's not leather. It looks like leather. It feels somewhat like leather and it's supposed to be easier to clean than real leather. To me there's not really a lot of difference. The brown cover is handsome and reminds me quite a lot of old leather bound novels. The color is just awesome. The fit is as good, maybe better, than my Amazon PaperWhite specific cover. I usually put my name, phone and email contacts under my Kindles. I couldn't get this OFF of my Kindle! I have bruises on several finger tips from trying. I may have to use a screw driver to carefully pry this off or I may need to cut this off if I decide to go back to my custom air-brushed Amazon cover. I'm going to leave it on for now. I've had black Kindle covers for years. This does everything the Amazon case does. There's decent protection for the Kindle. It has a magnetic clasp that automatically turns the Kindle on and off. It certainly holds the Kindle well enough. The cover folds back all the way for easy one handed reading. I'd have given it five stars but for one thing, the company thought it was a good idea to emboss their company name on the front cover. I just don't care for that. It's small so no one's going to see it. The only person who will see the embossed name is me and I've already got the product. I don't need to be constantly reminded who made it.<br /><br />{Edit - 06/13/16}<br />Just slightly more than a year since I obtained this case. I've left it on all this time because I really do like the brown color and the lack of the metal plate the Amazon case comes with. Fit's been perfect only had a couple issues where opening didn't turn the Paperwhite on but even the Amazon brand has that issue from time to time. My biggest problem is all the brown on the edges and corners either is or has already fallen off. The bulge where the magnet sits is beginning to split open. It's gotten some hard use and been with me in my walker bag for this past year but it hasn't worn well and while I still like the color I'm not sure I want to replace this with the same brand if, even at this price, it only lasts a year before falling apart. Makes me sad. Dropped from four to three stars.<br /><br />2nd Edit June 17th 2016 -<br />Inateck responded quickly to my review edited and comments about their product falling apart. I'm very impressed by their customer service which got the new cover to me very quickly even though I could find no warranty information for the product anywhere. They even allowed me to make a color change and select the red instead of the brown I had originally had. I hope this cover lasts longer than a year. Thank you for standing behind your product.<br /><br />I received this item in exchange for my review.

**翻译**：首先，我们来谈谈PU皮革的问题，查一下谷歌或维基百科，它不是皮革。它看起来像皮革。摸起来有点像皮革，而且据说比真皮更容易清洁。对我来说，其实没有太大区别。棕色封面很漂亮，让我想起很多旧皮革装订的小说。颜色真是太棒了。贴合度和我专门为亚马逊PaperWhite买的保护套一样好，甚至可能更好。我通常会把我的名字、电话和电子邮件联系方式放在我的Kindle下面。我无法把这个从我的Kindle上取下来！我试的时候几个指尖都弄出了瘀伤。我可能得用螺丝刀小心地撬开，或者如果我决定换回我定制的喷绘亚马逊保护套，我可能需要把它剪掉。我现在暂时留着它。我多年来一直用黑色Kindle保护套。这个做到了亚马逊保护套所做的一切。对Kindle有不错的保护。它有一个磁性搭扣，可以自动打开和关闭Kindle。它确实足够牢固地固定Kindle。封面可以完全向后折叠，方便单手阅读。我本来会给它五颗星，但有一件事，公司认为在封面上压印他们的公司名称是个好主意。我就是不喜欢这样。它很小，所以没人会看到。唯一会看到压印名字的人是我，而我已经买了这个产品。我不需要不断被提醒是谁做的。<br /><br />{编辑 - 2016年6月13日}<br />距离我获得这个保护套仅仅一年多一点。我一直留着它，因为我真的很喜欢棕色，而且没有亚马逊保护套附带的金属板。贴合度一直很完美，只有几次打开时没有打开Paperwhite，但即使是亚马逊品牌也时不时有这个问题。我最大的问题是边缘和角落的所有棕色要么正在脱落，要么已经脱落了。磁铁所在的地方开始裂开。它经历了一些重度使用，过去一年一直放在我的助行器包里，但它磨损得不好，虽然我仍然喜欢这个颜色，但我不确定是否想用同一个品牌替换它，如果即使在这个价格下，它也只能用一年就散架的话。让我难过。从四星降到三星。<br /><br />2016年6月17日第二次编辑 -<br />Inateck迅速回应了我的评论编辑和关于他们产品散架的评论。我对他们的客户服务印象深刻，他们很快就把新保护套寄给了我，尽管我在任何地方都找不到该产品的保修信息。他们甚至允许我改变颜色，选择红色而不是我原来的棕色。我希望这个保护套能用超过一年。谢谢你们支持自己的产品。<br /><br />我收到这个物品是为了换取我的评论。

**解释**：产品：Kindle保护套｜声音：评论者没有提到声音。｜其他：评论者讨论了PU皮革材质、棕色外观、贴合度、难以取下、保护性、磁性搭扣、折叠设计、压印公司名称、耐用性、客户服务以及换货。

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-036　G073

**原文**：I used about 90 feet of cat-6 cable to connect this.  Some of the cable is cat-6a, connected to cat-6 in the middle, so cat-6 is the standard I can count on.  It works for 4k@30, but not 4k@60 and not 3d.  I put cables in the wall when the room was being built, and didn't know that 50' HDMI cables that say they work don't always.  So I'm upset, but this helped fix the problem because I ran ethernet to both locations and a few extra cables too.  If this did 3d, I would be 100% happy.  Since it doesn't, I may have to put the blu-ray player up with the projector and figure out a way to return the audio instead of using this for it.  But this will work for the cable box and computer that are also connected.  So, in summary, if you want 3d and have Cat-6 cable, this doesn't work.  If 4k@30 is enough without 3d, then this is what you need and it works great!  In case you are curious, I tested using two HDMI switches too, and the 4k@30 survived going through both switches and this pair too!

**翻译**：我用了大约90英尺的cat-6网线来连接这个。有些线是cat-6a，中间连接着cat-6，所以cat-6是我能指望的标准。它适用于4k@30，但不适用于4k@60，也不支持3d。我在房间建造时就把线缆埋在了墙里，当时不知道标称能用的50英尺HDMI线并不总是能用。所以我很沮丧，但这帮助解决了问题，因为我在两个位置都布了以太网线，还多布了几根。如果这个能支持3d，我会100%满意。既然它不支持，我可能不得不把蓝光播放器放到投影仪旁边，并想办法把音频传回来，而不是用这个来传输音频。但这个可以用于同样连接的机顶盒和电脑。所以，总结一下，如果你想要3d并且有Cat-6线，这个不管用。如果4k@30就足够了且不需要3d，那么这就是你需要的，而且它工作得很好！如果你好奇的话，我还用了两个HDMI切换器进行测试，4k@30在经过两个切换器和这对设备后依然能正常工作！

**解释**：产品：HDMI over Cat-6 延长器｜声音：未提及声音｜其他：使用90英尺cat-6线缆，部分为cat-6a与cat-6混合；支持4k@30但不支持4k@60和3d；墙内预埋线缆；连接机顶盒和电脑；测试经过两个HDMI切换器后4k@30仍工作

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-007　G074

**原文**：Great set of Bluetooth earbuds.  When I am talking through the earbuds, my volume is normally on low. Even with a low setting, the people I am having a conversation can hear people across the street on the basketball courts.  It does pick up your surroundings well.  No static.  Very clear voice.  It is waterproof  I did shower with them on.  Only issue was that it turns off when the water hits it.  Unsure if it’s because it’s water or because it thinks I pushed them to turn it off. That’s why I am giving it a four star.

**翻译**：很棒的一套蓝牙耳机。当我通过耳机说话时，我的音量通常调得很低。即使设置很低，和我通话的人也能听到街对面篮球场上的人。它确实能很好地拾取周围环境的声音。没有静电。声音非常清晰。它是防水的，我戴着它洗过澡。唯一的问题是，当水碰到它时它会关机。不确定是因为水，还是因为它以为我按了它们来关机。这就是我给它四星的原因。

**解释**：产品：蓝牙耳机｜声音：通话时音量通常调低，但对方能听到街对面篮球场的人（the people I am having a conversation can hear people across the street on the basketball courts），能很好地拾取周围环境（It does pick up your surroundings well），没有静电（No static），声音非常清晰（Very clear voice）｜其他：防水，戴着洗澡（waterproof, I did shower with them on）；水碰到时会关机（it turns off when the water hits it），不确定是水还是误触关机（Unsure if it’s because it’s water or because it thinks I pushed them to turn it off）；因此给四星（giving it a four star）

Have to find some other brand for a tiny woman's head.

## S2-091　G080

**原文**：I had a bad experience with my previous Annke security DVR. I could never get it set up with the online viewer, couldn't back it up, and eventually couldn't do a thing with it when the USB port stopped working (you need a mouse). The way I found out the port was no longer working was when I went to review footage of a creep on my porch. Couldn't do it.<br /><br />So far, this one is lots better. It seems to have a better build quality, and, while set up was not easy, it was doable.<br /><br />I'm really tired of stuff not working - especially security equipment that, at best, is just okay, and, at worst, does no good whatsoever. If this keeps working, I'll be thrilled.

## S2-034　G082

**原文**：I recently purchased a new zoom lens for my camera after I broke the old one when I took a tumble. I had filters like these for my old lens but the thread size was different so my old filters were no longer useful. I selected this filter for my new lens, somewhat apprehensively because of the relatively low asking price. It has proven to be a decent filter but it is not useful in all situations.<br /><br />The idea behind this filter is light diffusion which will give an image a very slight haze or softness, depending on the lighting and subject. This is an effect that can be emulated in software during post-processing but in doing this type of enhancement I have found it is sometimes unnatural. It certainly is less fun than making it happen while shooting, which is why these types of filters are available.<br /><br />In use it works, though I have had mixed results. When using it with certain light sources, the filter seems to have no real effect at all, while with others it creates a subtle, soft glow around the subject. I am still working out the situations where it is most effective. I have had similar issues with filters like these with other lenses, and as such I assume the problem is in my lack of practice with them. It will take some time and practice to make my results more consistent, and for now I lay the fault on myself.<br /><br />The build quality seems to be good. I have received filters in the past where the glass rattles in the frame which is unsettling. This one is tight and there is no play between the glass and the frame. It screws on to the lens securely and is easy to clean.<br /><br />The asking price is quite reasonable. I have seen these filters priced much higher, and I have also seen much cheaper ones that do not work well. The pricing seems in line with the product, if not slightly low.<br /><br />I have no complaints with this filter. The issues I have with it are probably my inexperience, and I will keep working with it to improve my results. I will return here and update my review if my impression changes. For now, though, I am satisfied.

**翻译**：我最近为相机买了一个新的变焦镜头，因为之前摔了一跤把旧镜头摔坏了。我旧镜头有类似的滤镜，但螺纹尺寸不同，所以旧滤镜不再有用了。我为新镜头选择了这款滤镜，由于价格相对较低，我有些忐忑。事实证明它是一款不错的滤镜，但并非在所有情况下都有用。<br /><br />这款滤镜的原理是光线扩散，会根据光线和拍摄对象给图像带来非常轻微的雾感或柔化效果。这种效果可以在后期处理时用软件模拟，但我在做这类增强时发现有时会不自然。当然，拍摄时直接实现比后期处理更有趣，这也是这类滤镜存在的原因。<br /><br />在使用中它确实有效，不过我得到的效果参差不齐。在某些光源下使用时，滤镜似乎完全没有效果，而在其他光源下则会在拍摄对象周围产生微妙的柔和光晕。我仍在摸索它最有效的情境。我在其他镜头上使用类似滤镜时也遇到过类似问题，因此我认为问题在于我缺乏练习。需要一些时间和练习才能让我的效果更稳定，目前我把问题归咎于自己。<br /><br />做工质量似乎不错。我以前收到过玻璃在框架中晃动的滤镜，那让人不安。这款很紧，玻璃和框架之间没有松动。它牢固地拧在镜头上，也容易清洁。<br /><br />要价相当合理。我见过这些滤镜定价高得多，也见过便宜得多但效果不好的。定价似乎与产品相符，甚至略低。<br /><br />我对这款滤镜没有不满。我对它的问题可能是我经验不足，我会继续使用它来改善效果。如果我的印象改变，我会回来更新评论。不过目前，我是满意的。

**解释**：产品：相机滤镜（光线扩散滤镜）｜声音：未提及声音相关内容。｜其他：提及了购买新变焦镜头、滤镜的螺纹尺寸、光线扩散效果、使用效果参差不齐、做工质量、价格合理、以及个人经验不足等内容。

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S3-039　G085

**原文**：The anti-glare feature of this blue light blocking screen has reduced the strain I was experiencing with my eyes.  My doctor suggested I buy one. I never realized I could use this.<br /><br />You don’t attach it to your computer screen. You just hang it over your screen. It slides into position easily and it’s a great fit.<br /><br />I’m happy with it!

**翻译**：这款防蓝光屏幕的防眩光功能减轻了我之前眼睛的疲劳感。我的医生建议我买一个。我从未意识到我可以使用这个。<br /><br />你不需要把它贴在电脑屏幕上。你只需把它挂在屏幕上方。它很容易滑入位置，而且非常合适。<br /><br />我对它很满意！

**解释**：产品：防蓝光屏幕｜声音：未提及声音｜其他：防眩光功能、减轻眼睛疲劳、医生建议、安装方式（挂在屏幕上、滑入位置、合适）、满意度

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S1-012　G086

**原文**：It ultimately depends on your use case, but I'd say the juice isn't worth the squeeze here. The product itself works fine, but not necessarily much better or worse than other 1080p camera's. The feature set is low for the price point though everything works well here, the features driving up the price don't make this camera feel as though it's worth double the price of other 1080p camera's.<br /><br />If you happen to need a speaker built into your webcam, which I'm sure is where part of the premium price lies, then you don't have many better options. This issue is, how limited is that use case? Most people's computers have speakers and you could likely get an equivalent webcam and better speakers for the same price.<br /><br />One of the things that bothered me, and one of the reasons I wanted to review the item, is the confusing nature of the marketing at play here. Putting 2k and 1080p next to each other is deceptive at best, as they're trying to indicate it has a 2k auto focus sensor but using verbiage that makes it seem like this might be a 1440p camera (which is occasionally referred to as 2k) to the shopper that doesn't ready all the details.<br /><br />I can confirm this camera is only 1080p and, one of the things really hurting it at this price point, only appears to offer a maximum of 30fps.<br /><br />Does it work? Yes. It works relatively well even. For this price though, you can do better.

**翻译**：最终取决于你的使用场景，但我觉得这里得不偿失。产品本身运行良好，但不一定比其他1080p摄像头好多少或差多少。虽然价格点上的功能集较低，但这里一切运行良好，推高价格的功能并不让这款摄像头感觉值得比其他1080p摄像头贵一倍。<br /><br />如果你恰好需要网络摄像头内置扬声器，我相信这是部分溢价所在，那么你没有多少更好的选择。问题是，这种使用场景有多有限？大多数人的电脑都有扬声器，你很可能以同样的价格获得一个等效的网络摄像头和更好的扬声器。<br /><br />困扰我的一件事，也是我想评论这个物品的原因之一，是这里营销的混乱性质。将2k和1080p放在一起充其量是欺骗性的，因为他们试图表明它有一个2k自动对焦传感器，但使用的措辞让不阅读所有细节的购物者觉得这可能是一个1440p摄像头（偶尔被称为2k）。<br /><br />我可以确认这款摄像头只有1080p，而且在这个价格点上真正伤害它的一件事是，它似乎只提供最高30fps。<br /><br />它工作吗？是的。它甚至运行得相对良好。但对于这个价格，你可以做得更好。

**解释**：产品：网络摄像头｜声音：评论者没有提及声音。｜其他：评论者讨论了产品的价值、功能集、内置扬声器、营销策略、分辨率（1080p vs 2k）、帧率（30fps）以及价格点。

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-035　G089

**原文**：The Belkin Miracast Video Adapter works well if the correct equipment is available, but doesn't work at all if you are using a noncompliant smartphone or television.  I first thought my two year old Vizio television would not work because it has no USB port, but after ordering a new set, realized that the USB could be utilized from an outside source.  After trying several times to ate my Razr to the Belkin, I realized that the connection was unobtainable because the phone is not operating on the correct system.  I then enlisted a friend with a compatible phone and we were able to make the connection very quickly, then watched gleefully on the Vizio.  For a smartphone with Miracast capability, this works well and should be considered.  Since technology changes rapidly, as do our phones, I would not hesitate to use this if it fits into your budget.  When I replace my smartphone in the next few months, I will certainly verify on the Belkin website that the new purchase is compatible because it is an outstanding option for viewing images and listening to audio on a big screen.  I have been satisfied with my other Belkin products and find them to be a reliable company.  If your set has an HDMI port which isn't easy to access, this device also includes an extender which has worked well for my set.  Otherwise, I would not have been able to plug in the USB cable because of its position.

**翻译**：Belkin Miracast 视频适配器在具备正确设备的情况下运行良好，但如果你使用的是不符合标准的智能手机或电视，则完全无法工作。我起初以为我那台用了两年的 Vizio 电视无法使用，因为它没有 USB 端口，但在订购了一台新设备后，意识到 USB 可以从外部来源使用。在多次尝试将我的 Razr 连接到 Belkin 后，我意识到连接无法实现，因为手机没有运行正确的系统。然后我找了一位拥有兼容手机的朋友，我们很快就建立了连接，然后在 Vizio 上高兴地观看。对于具备 Miracast 功能的智能手机，这款产品运行良好，值得考虑。由于技术变化迅速，我们的手机也是如此，如果它符合你的预算，我会毫不犹豫地使用它。当我在未来几个月更换智能手机时，我一定会先在 Belkin 网站上验证新购买的设备是否兼容，因为它是在大屏幕上观看图像和收听音频的出色选择。我对其他 Belkin 产品一直很满意，并认为他们是一家可靠的公司。如果你的电视有一个不易接触的 HDMI 端口，这个设备还附带了一个延长器，对我的电视很有效。否则，由于 USB 电缆的位置，我将无法插入它。

**解释**：产品：Belkin Miracast 视频适配器｜声音：提到在大屏幕上收听音频（listening to audio on a big screen），未对音质作具体描述。｜其他：兼容性要求（需要符合标准的智能手机或电视、Miracast 功能）、USB 端口和 HDMI 端口的使用、延长器、品牌可靠性、预算考虑、未来更换手机时验证兼容性。

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-029　G090

**原文**：I purchased this for two specific reasons: (1) safety because too many accidents are caused by drivers with a phone to their ear, and (2) I love music and my Droid X has become my MP3 player (among other things as well as a phone.<br /><br />Installation was a snap, although I did have to read the directions, which were clear and easy to follow. In less than 5 minutes after removing it from the packaging I was listening to music through my car radio!<br /><br />This device comes with a fairly short USB charging cable that connects to a port on the head. The length made it inconvenient, and it was pretty flimsy too, failing after about three months. I added a [[ASIN:B0042X8XQO PowerLine 90310 Auto Socket Splitter with USB Power Port]] (which cleared my console in my 2008 Mazda 3 with the GOgroove and [[ASIN:B000S5Q9CA Motorola Vehicle Power Adapter micro-USB Rapid Rate Charger]] plugged in), and the combination, while unwieldy, has worked perfectly ever since.<br /><br />Music and calls are reasonably clear. I do have to turn my radio volume up when a call comes in, but that may be an issue with my phone and not the GOgroove. And, like a hands-free system should work, when there is an incoming call or you wish to make an outgoing one, the MP3 player feature in my Droid X pauses the music until I am finished using the phone. After calls are terminated any paused music will automatically resume.<br /><br />For long trips where you pass through areas with radio stations, your clear signal set in one location may start to degrade and you will notice static. That is caused by you coming into the range of a strong FM signal that is competing with the one you selected. You will need to seek and switch to a new frequency when that happens. For example, if I am traveling from, say, Ormond Beach, FL to Jacksonville, FL, I will need to change my frequency twice going up and twice again returning. It's the nature of any such device and there is nothing that can be designed into the GOgroove to fix it - at least not at the price point at which this device is selling.<br /><br />I've had friends connect to it with their iPhones, and it works without a hitch. The icing on the cake is the GOgroove remains paired with my Droid X, and automatically connects to it as soon as I start my car if I am playing music. This is a convenient feature that I wish my [[ASIN:B0047T79VS Belkin F8Z492TTP Bluetooth Music Receiver]] that is connected to my home stereo had - I have to manually connect to that device anytime I want to use it.<br /><br />Overall I am thrilled with my GOgroove and if it dies I would not hesitate to purchase another. In fact, I am considering purchasing a spare anyway because I spend a lot of time in my car and life without this wonderful gizmo would not be the same.

**翻译**：我购买这个产品有两个具体原因：（1）安全，因为太多事故是由司机把手机贴在耳边造成的；（2）我热爱音乐，而我的Droid X已经成为我的MP3播放器（除了手机之外还有其他功能）。安装非常简单，尽管我确实需要阅读说明书，说明书清晰易懂。从包装中取出后不到5分钟，我就在通过汽车收音机听音乐了！这个设备配有一根相当短的USB充电线，连接到耳机头上的一个端口。长度不方便，而且也很脆弱，大约三个月后就坏了。我添加了一个[[ASIN:B0042X8XQO PowerLine 90310 Auto Socket Splitter with USB Power Port]]（这清理了我2008年马自达3的中控台，插入了GOgroove和[[ASIN:B000S5Q9CA Motorola Vehicle Power Adapter micro-USB Rapid Rate Charger]]），这个组合虽然笨重，但从那以后一直工作得很好。音乐和通话相当清晰。来电时我确实需要调高收音机音量，但这可能是我的手机的问题，而不是GOgroove的问题。而且，就像免提系统应该工作的那样，当有来电或你想拨出电话时，我的Droid X中的MP3播放器功能会暂停音乐，直到我打完电话。通话结束后，任何暂停的音乐都会自动恢复。对于长途旅行，当你经过有电台的区域时，你在一个位置设置的清晰信号可能会开始退化，你会注意到静电。这是因为你进入了强FM信号的范围内，与您选择的信号竞争。当这种情况发生时，你需要寻找并切换到一个新的频率。例如，如果我从佛罗里达州奥蒙德海滩到佛罗里达州杰克逊维尔，我需要在去程切换两次频率，回程再切换两次。这是任何此类设备的特性，GOgroove无法设计出任何东西来解决这个问题——至少在这个设备销售的价格点上不能。我有朋友用他们的iPhone连接它，工作毫无障碍。锦上添花的是，GOgroove保持与我的Droid X配对，并且如果我正在播放音乐，一启动汽车就会自动连接到它。这是一个方便的功能，我希望我连接到家立体声的[[ASIN:B0047T79VS Belkin F8Z492TTP Bluetooth Music Receiver]]也有——我每次想使用那个设备时都必须手动连接。总的来说，我对我的GOgroove非常满意，如果它坏了，我会毫不犹豫地再买一个。事实上，我考虑无论如何都买一个备用，因为我花很多时间在车里，没有这个奇妙的小工具，生活将不一样。

**解释**：产品：GOgroove 蓝牙车载免提套件｜声音：音乐和通话相当清晰（Music and calls are reasonably clear）；来电时需调高收音机音量（I do have to turn my radio volume up when a call comes in）；长途旅行中信号可能退化并出现静电（your clear signal set in one location may start to degrade and you will notice static）；需要切换频率（You will need to seek and switch to a new frequency）｜其他：安装简单（Installation was a snap）；USB充电线短且脆弱（fairly short USB charging cable... pretty flimsy too, failing after about three months）；添加了电源分配器和充电器（added a PowerLine 90310 Auto Socket Splitter with USB Power Port and Motorola Vehicle Power Adapter）；自动连接和配对（automatically connects to it as soon as I start my car）；与iPhone兼容（friends connect to it with their iPhones）；与Belkin蓝牙音乐接收器比较（compared to Belkin F8Z492TTP Bluetooth Music Receiver）；整体满意并考虑购买备用（Overall I am thrilled... considering purchasing a spare）

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

**原文**：I bought this because the speaker on my phone died but the headphone jack still worked.  It was still somewhat inconvenient and used battery very quickly.  The sound was also not really very loud and I still missed calls even when there was relatively quiet ambient noise, as in, alone in the house with no radio, TV, and other sounds in a quiet neighborhood. ## S1-042　G095

**原文**：Pros: Plenty of room in this case for most builds.  I bought the NZXT vertical gpu mount and it works great with a Gigabyte RTX 4080 Gaming OC. It arrived locally 2 days before the scheduled arrival date (which was about 1 week) and they held it at the warehouse until the delivery date.  So much for Prime shipping.

## S1-001　G097

**原文**：So this is the 2nd bookbook I have owned. I had an original model for my ipad, and felt - and still do feel - that it is one of the best ipad cases on the market. Real leather outer, suede inner. A zipper that worked.  Sure the system used to restrain your ipad was not that great, but it was functional. Until one of my corner straps broke, leading me to ordering a replacement.<br /><br />The new model has a much better setup to hold the ipad in place. But that is about the only thing going for it. The exterior feels like sandpaper, and if it is leather ( which I don't think it is) the dye job is horrible. The zipper is so flimsy and finicky that I am afraid to open and close it. I am sure that will be the first point of failure on this.<br /><br />This used to be a great product. I don't understand what happened with this case. I have owned 3 different iphone cases, and two of the original ipad cases and felt they couldn't be beat. This case makes me feel that I was beat and robbed.<br /><br />Don'y buy it.

**翻译**：所以这是我拥有的第二个bookbook。我之前有一个用于我的iPad的原始型号，并且觉得——现在仍然觉得——它是市场上最好的iPad保护壳之一。真皮外层，麂皮内衬。一个能用的拉链。当然，用来固定iPad的系统不是很好，但它是可用的。直到我的一个角带断了，导致我订购了一个替换品。<br /><br />新型号有一个更好的设置来固定iPad。但这几乎是它唯一的优点。外部感觉像砂纸，如果它是皮革（我不认为它是），染色工作很糟糕。拉链如此脆弱和挑剔，我害怕打开和关闭它。我相信这将是这个产品的第一个故障点。<br /><br />这曾经是一个很棒的产品。我不明白这个保护壳发生了什么。我拥有过3个不同的iPhone保护壳，和两个原始的iPad保护壳，觉得它们无与伦比。这个保护壳让我觉得我被击败和抢劫了。<br /><br />不要买它。

**解释**：产品：iPad保护壳（bookbook品牌）｜声音：未提及声音相关内容｜其他：材质（真皮外层、麂皮内衬、外部像砂纸、染色差）、拉链（脆弱、挑剔、可能首先故障）、iPad固定系统（新型号更好）、角带断裂、购买替换品、与旧型号对比、不建议购买

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-031　G098

**原文**：This LaCie 2TB USB-C hard drive is light, small and very sleek looking.  Other reviewers have mentioned the drive's sharp edges which can scratch a table or couch, so that's one thing to take be aware of when using it.  I found the HD easy to set up and use with my Mac.  I set it up to save files and to allow for the Time Machine backup.  The HD was quiet, has a light that shows its progress and that it is saving files which is reassuring.  While the LaCie 2TB USB-C is pricey,  it does offer USB-C connectivity as well as a good looking product.

**翻译**：这款LaCie 2TB USB-C硬盘轻便、小巧，外观非常时尚。其他评论者提到过这款硬盘的锋利边缘可能会刮伤桌子或沙发，所以使用时需要注意这一点。我发现这款硬盘很容易在我的Mac上设置和使用。我将其设置为保存文件并允许Time Machine备份。这款硬盘很安静，有一个指示灯显示进度和正在保存文件，这让人很安心。虽然LaCie 2TB USB-C价格昂贵，但它确实提供了USB-C连接以及好看的产品外观。

**解释**：产品：LaCie 2TB USB-C硬盘｜声音：评论者说这款硬盘很安静（quiet），有一个指示灯显示进度和正在保存文件（has a light that shows its progress and that it is saving files）。｜其他：评论者提到这款硬盘轻便、小巧、外观时尚（light, small and very sleek looking）；其他评论者提到锋利边缘可能刮伤桌子或沙发（sharp edges which can scratch a table or couch）；在Mac上容易设置和使用（easy to set up and use with my Mac）；设置为保存文件和Time Machine备份（save files and to allow for the Time Machine backup）；价格昂贵（pricey）；提供USB-C连接（USB-C connectivity）；外观好看（good looking product）。

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-050　G099

**原文**：I wanted a smaller device as a backup to my iPad2, so I bought this secondhand. It was easy to navigate around the contents. I added a N2A(Nook to Android) 32GB microSD card so I can also access android apps and have more storage. Amazon has the best price for that and after installing the card, I can get more out of this device. It was a little frustrating at first to navigate around the android OS but it has been worth it. Sometimes my iPad2 is too large to carry around, and the Nook Color easily slips inside my purse. I am using this mainly as an ereader and note taker. Maybe later I might install some free games. Putting on an antiglare screen helped a lot for when I am in bright light settings. The color and clarity of the content is really nice. I gave this only 4 stars because I think the OS could still use some tweaking. Overall I am pleased.

**翻译**：我想要一个更小的设备作为我iPad2的备用，所以我买了这个二手的。浏览内容很容易。我加了一张N2A（Nook转Android）32GB microSD卡，这样我也可以访问安卓应用并有更多存储空间。亚马逊的价格最好，安装卡后，我可以从这个设备中获得更多。一开始在安卓操作系统上导航有点令人沮丧，但这是值得的。有时我的iPad2太大，不方便携带，而Nook Color可以轻松放进我的手提包。我主要用它作为电子阅读器和笔记工具。也许以后我会安装一些免费游戏。贴上防眩光屏幕在明亮光线下帮助很大。内容的颜色和清晰度真的很好。我只给了4星，因为我认为操作系统仍然需要一些调整。总体而言，我很满意。

**解释**：产品：Nook Color 电子阅读器｜声音：未提及｜其他：提及了设备尺寸、二手购买、导航便利性、N2A 32GB microSD卡、安卓应用、存储扩展、亚马逊价格、iPad2对比、手提包携带、电子阅读和笔记用途、免费游戏、防眩光屏幕、内容颜色和清晰度、操作系统调整、总体满意度。

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-071　G102

**原文**：You know, when before this board was to be released, it was rumored to be at the $200 price point.  If you purchased quality PBT keycaps and an aluminum case separately, you would already exceed the price.  This is probably the best keyboard you are going to get for $130 dollars.<br /><br />This is my first keyboard with PBT keycaps and while it gives the feel of quality, I'm not sure If I prefer ABS instead. There are several layers of programming possible, and you can program any key to just about any other key, or macro key combinations.  Ultimately, this board has more features than I will ever need.  I like that there are no feet; it gives typing a very sturdy feel.  Unless you like typing at a very high incline, but that would be easy enough to make minor modifications to elevate it.<br /><br />If you are looking at this then you are probably also looking at the KBP V60, which costs virtually the same.  The only reason to get that board is you prefer illumination, ABS keycaps, don't need the programmability, and prefer a lighter board.  I also purchased the V60 and am waiting for it to be delivered.  I want to make sure that I am making the right choice.<br /><br />Other boards to consider are the Choc Mini, although this is a lower class board, it may meet your needs.  There is the Ducky Mini which has an aluminum bezel which makes it larger which kind of defeats the purpose of the 60% board IMHO.  There is the Tex Beetle.  These are all at a similar price point and similar layout.  I will post back after I have had the V60 for a few days.  Who knows, maybe I will actually prefer it.  I'm not 100% sure I like the PBT keycaps as they feel like typing on cubes of concrete, although I would be in the minority.  Also worth mentioning is that the space bar kind of creaks when it is depressed.  I'm not sure if it is the stabilizer, spring, or defect, but it is mildly annoying. (One more thing I want to mention is that my favorite mechanical keyboard ever is the Cherry MX Board 3.0.  It is more of an economy board, but the keystrokes are the smoothest, the board is quieter, and it has great low profile keys which really do make typing smoother.  I don't know why these have not caught on).<br /><br />Edit: I wan to add that have since purchsed the Poker 3 LED.  It gives typing a different feel.  In comparison, the PBT keycaps make the keystrokes feel stiff and mushy.  I actually prefer the LED version with the ABS.  They are fairly high quality, not the crap you get on a typical Dell keyboard.  My preference for ABS goes against the grain as most prefer PBT, but this is all subjective.

**翻译**：你知道，在这块板子发布之前，有传闻说它的价格会在200美元左右。如果你单独购买优质的PBT键帽和铝制外壳，价格就已经超过了。这可能是你在130美元能买到的最好的键盘了。<br /><br />这是我第一把带有PBT键帽的键盘，虽然它给人一种高品质的感觉，但我不确定我是否更喜欢ABS。有多层编程可能，你可以将任何键编程为几乎任何其他键，或宏键组合。最终，这块板子的功能比我需要的还要多。我喜欢它没有脚；这给打字一种非常稳固的感觉。除非你喜欢在非常高的倾斜度下打字，但那很容易通过小的修改来抬高它。<br /><br />如果你在看这个，那么你可能也在看KBP V60，它的价格几乎相同。选择那块板子的唯一理由是：你喜欢背光、ABS键帽、不需要编程功能，并且喜欢更轻的板子。我也购买了V60，正在等待它送达。我想确保我做出了正确的选择。<br /><br />其他可以考虑的板子有Choc Mini，虽然这是低一档的板子，但它可能满足你的需求。还有Ducky Mini，它有铝制边框，使其更大，这有点违背了60%板子的目的，恕我直言。还有Tex Beetle。这些都在相似的价格点和相似的布局。等我用V60几天后会再发帖。谁知道呢，也许我实际上会更喜欢它。我不是100%确定我喜欢PBT键帽，因为它们感觉像在混凝土块上打字，尽管我会是少数派。另外值得一提的是，空格键在按下时有点吱吱作响。我不确定是稳定器、弹簧还是缺陷，但这有点烦人。（还有一件事我想提一下，我最喜欢的机械键盘是Cherry MX Board 3.0。它更像是一块经济型板子，但击键最平滑，板子更安静，而且它有很棒的低profile键，确实让打字更顺畅。我不知道为什么这些没有流行起来）。<br /><br />编辑：我想补充一下，我后来购买了Poker 3 LED。它给打字一种不同的感觉。相比之下，PBT键帽使击键感觉僵硬和软绵。我实际上更喜欢带有ABS的LED版本。它们质量相当高，不是你在典型Dell键盘上得到的那种垃圾。我对ABS的偏好与主流相反，因为大多数人更喜欢PBT，但这一切都是主观的。

**解释**：产品：机械键盘｜声音：空格键在按下时有点吱吱作响（space bar kind of creaks when it is depressed）；不确定是稳定器、弹簧还是缺陷（not sure if it is the stabilizer, spring, or defect）；有点烦人（mildly annoying）；Cherry MX Board 3.0的板子更安静（the board is quieter）｜其他：价格（$200传闻，$130实际）；PBT键帽与ABS键帽的偏好（PBT感觉像在混凝土块上打字，更喜欢ABS）；编程功能（多层编程，可编程任何键）；无脚设计（稳固的打字感觉）；与其他键盘比较（KBP V60、Choc Mini、Ducky Mini、Tex Beetle、Poker 3 LED）；购买V60并等待送达；Cherry MX Board 3.0的击键平滑和低profile键

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S3-024　G104

**原文**：That's a line from a Gin Blossoms song, that fit's these speakers. I wasn't looking for something to sound great, just something that didn't sound terrible, was small, and I didn't need another A/C power cord to run. This fits the bill and they will work when I just need a temporary or non-musical speaker.<br /><br />Otherwise, the sound is tinny and mediocre. They will work for what I need them for, just don't expect great sound.<br /><br />PS-Read all the other reviews about the annoying lights and didn't think it was a big deal. My new PC powers the USB ports even when the computer is off, so these lights are on 24/7 unless I unplug them from the computer. Annoying and useless.

**翻译**：那是Gin Blossoms乐队歌曲里的一句歌词，正好适合这些音箱。我并不是在找音质很棒的东西，只是想要一个听起来不太糟糕、体积小、而且不需要再插一根交流电源线的音箱。这个符合要求，当我只需要一个临时或非音乐用途的音箱时，它们能派上用场。<br /><br />除此之外，声音单薄刺耳、平庸。它们能满足我的需求，只是别指望有很好的音质。<br /><br />附注——看了所有其他关于烦人灯光的评论，本来觉得不是什么大事。我的新电脑即使关机也给USB端口供电，所以除非我把它们从电脑上拔下来，否则这些灯24小时都亮着。烦人又没用。

**解释**：产品：音箱｜声音：声音单薄刺耳、平庸（tinny and mediocre），能满足需求但别指望有很好的音质（don't expect great sound）｜其他：提到Gin Blossoms歌曲歌词、购买需求（不追求音质好、体积小、不需要额外交流电源线）、临时或非音乐用途、USB端口持续供电导致灯光24小时亮着、觉得灯光烦人又没用

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S1-094　G107

**原文**：I ordered the 'Emart Heavy Duty C Stand Max Height 10ft /300cm, Adjustable Photography Stand with 4.2ft/128cm Holding Arm 2 Pieces Grip Head and Baby Pin for Photography Reflector, Softbox, Monolight' for review and would like to share some of my thoughts.<br /><br />I have been using this C Stand for some time now and I would like to first say that this is my very first C Stand so I don't have anything to compare it with but I can say that this thing is a beast and is very sturdy. It was very simple to set up and I appreciate the fact that it comes with the Baby Pin, which I use all the time with this stand. My only complaint is that adjusting the arms can be a bit of a pain as you really need to loosen them quite a bit, then pull them apart manually to separate the teeth to allow the arm to freely move. Not having had or used a C Stand before, I am not sure if this is a common issue with other brands or not, but I would think that there would be a spring in the arm mechanics that would push the teeth apart when loosening the clamps and make the adjustments a bit easier.<br />With that said, I still love this C Stand and would highly recommend it, as it is very well built aside from the clamps issue. It is as described, and very heavy duty indeed.

**翻译**：我订购了‘Emart重型C型支架，最大高度10英尺/300厘米，可调节摄影支架，带4.2英尺/128厘米横臂、两个夹头和婴儿销，用于摄影反光板、柔光箱、单灯’来评测，并想分享一些我的想法。<br /><br />我已经使用这个C型支架有一段时间了，我想首先说这是我第一个C型支架，所以我没有其他东西可以比较，但我可以说这东西是个巨无霸，非常坚固。它设置起来非常简单，我很感激它附带婴儿销，我一直在这个支架上使用它。我唯一的抱怨是调整横臂可能有点麻烦，因为你真的需要把它们松开很多，然后手动拉开它们以分开齿，让横臂自由移动。由于以前没有或使用过C型支架，我不确定这是否是其他品牌的常见问题，但我认为横臂机械结构中应该有一个弹簧，在松开夹具时推开齿，使调整更容易一些。<br />话虽如此，我仍然喜欢这个C型支架，并强烈推荐它，因为除了夹具问题外，它建造得非常好。它如描述的那样，确实非常重型。

**解释**：产品：不确定｜声音：（仅翻译，无解析：该条反复触发中性闸门，按兜底策略只提供翻译）｜其他：

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-070　G108

**原文**：I ordered this lens and filter kit to go with my Samsung NX300 with the intent of expanding the capabilities of the camera without spending a fortune. I looked at several different kits before settling on this one, based on the price. I will admit to being a bit apprehensive about the cost in relation to the number of lenses and filters in the kit (presently this kit sells for less than $35 on Amazon as I write this) but my concern was ill-placed - this is a good kit.<br /><br />I will start off by saying that I am not a professional photographer by any stretch of the imagination. I could be best described as an enthusiast who is trying to learn as I go.<br /><br />I have experimented and played with many of the various components with this kit, starting with the 2.2x telephoto lens. This is a chunky item, which should be expected as it is a glass lens. It attached without issue to my 18mm-55mm OIS lens and I was able to use it almost immediately out of the box. It took me a little fiddling at first to get the focus just right as I am still learning with the camera but once I figured out the manual focus I was able to improve the zoom range with the telephoto lens in place. This, to me, is worth the cost of the kit. One thing I did notice, however, is that when the telescoping zoom is not fully extended on the regular lens, this lens adds a shadowy blackout area around the perimeter of the image that fades when the zoom is telescoped out.<br /><br />My favorite component of this kit is the .43x wide-angle lens which works surprisingly well with the camera's included lens. It is another chunky item like the telephoto lens and it attaches easily as well. Even at closer ranges (15 feet or so) it allows the camera to capture appreciably more than the regular lens, without distortion. This is probably the one component I will use more than the others, and again this is an accessory that is worth the cost of the kit. This lens is not going to give the same quality result as a dedicated wide-angle lens but the price/quality tradeoff is worth it.<br /><br />There are four close-up lenses in this kit that vary in strength. These are fun to play with though I did start to get a lot of edge distortion when using the strongest lens. This is not a problem as there are times when this kind of distortion is a good thing. I was able to get similar close-up shots - without distortion - with the Samsung lens but it required a lot more effort. Interestingly, one of these lenses had a smudge on it when I removed it from it's cellophane envelope. A quick wipe with a microfiber cloth did a fine job of cleaning the lens but this puzzled me - why was it smudged?<br /><br />The wide-angle lens comes coupled with a macro lens that is absolutely astounding for close-up shots. I use this lens for taking pictures of pattern weaves, close-ups of surfaces, animal fur and other interesting subjects (the knee of my denim jeans makes a really interesting background shot). It works even a little better than the close-up lenses and is likely going to be the only lens I use for when I need a really detailed close-in shot of something. Though this lens outperforms the close-up magnifying lenses it requires decoupling from the wide-angle lens before it can be used and this is a minor hassle.<br /><br />The kit includes a UV filter, a CPL filter and a FL-D filter. I used the CPL filter while experimenting and the result was minorly disappointing but I figured that was due to unrealistic expectations. It did tweak the color profile just a little but not dramatically like I had hoped. The FL-D filter was another story and gave the whole picture a completely different color profile. It is designed for use with fluorescent lighting (which means it should be used a lot when indoors) so when it is used with incandescent lighting it give a very eerie and unusual hue to pictures. With my lens I can adjust for fluorescent lighting electronically so this isn't a lens I necessarily need but I still like to mess with it. I have not tried the UV filter.<br /><br />The cleaning kit is an important item in the kit, for removing dirt and smudges from the various lenses without using a harsh cloth. It's very basic but is a fine start - cleaning spray, cotton swabs, a microfiber cloth and a squeeze duster for puffing away particles when a full cleaning is not needed.<br /><br />This kit includes a couple of other miscellaneous items as well, including a miniature tripod for a desk mount and a 6-compartment memory card storage case. These are nice-to-have extras, though I confess I am thoroughly unimpressed with the tripod which is a flexible bendy-leg tripod that does not retain the position in which it is set when supporting the camera, so it really is of little use. I continue to mess with the tripod, though, as I am sure I can get it to work in certain scenarios.<br /><br />The main negative I encountered so far was actually the manner in which the kit was packaged and shipped to me. Everything was in a large plastic envelope bag with no padding at all. What would prevent the items in the kit from being crushed in such a shipment? Fortunately, all items were individually wrapped in small and sturdy cardboard boxes and one of those boxes was actually slightly crushed though there was no damage to the merchandise inside. Something like this should be shipped in a larger corrugated box, to better protect everything.<br /><br />If you have a Samsung camera for which this kit is compatible, this is a very good kit to extend the capability of the camera without breaking the bank. The various lenses add range and effects, the filters are nice to have on hand and the extras are icing on the cake. At the current pricing, this is a very good deal.

**翻译**：我订购了这款镜头和滤镜套装，用来搭配我的三星NX300，目的是在不花大钱的情况下扩展相机的功能。在决定买这款之前，我看了好几款不同的套装，最终基于价格选择了这一款。我承认对于这个价格与套装中镜头和滤镜数量的关系有点担心（目前我写这条评论时，这套装在亚马逊上售价不到35美元），但我的担心是多余的——这是一款不错的套装。<br /><br />首先我要说，我绝对不是什么专业摄影师。最贴切的描述是，我是一个边学边玩的爱好者。<br /><br />我尝试并玩过这套装中的许多不同组件，从2.2倍长焦镜头开始。这是一个厚实的家伙，考虑到它是玻璃镜头，这也在意料之中。它毫无问题地安装在我的18mm-55mm OIS镜头上，我几乎一开箱就能使用。起初我花了一点时间调整对焦，因为我还在学习使用相机，但一旦我弄明白了手动对焦，装上长焦镜头后就能改善变焦范围。对我来说，这已经值回套装的价钱了。不过，我注意到一件事：当普通镜头的伸缩变焦没有完全伸出时，这个镜头会在图像周围增加一个阴影般的黑色区域，当变焦伸出时这个区域会消失。<br /><br />这套装中我最喜欢的组件是0.43倍广角镜头，它与相机附带的镜头配合得出奇地好。它和长焦镜头一样是个厚实的家伙，也很容易安装。即使在较近的距离（大约15英尺），它也能让相机捕捉到比普通镜头明显更多的画面，而且没有畸变。这可能是我会比其它组件用得更多的一个，而且这又是一个值回套装价钱的配件。这个镜头不会给出与专用广角镜头相同质量的结果，但价格与质量的权衡是值得的。<br /><br />这套装中有四个不同强度的近摄镜。它们玩起来很有趣，尽管使用最强的那片时我开始遇到很多边缘畸变。这不是问题，因为有时候这种畸变是件好事。我用三星镜头也能拍到类似的近摄照片——没有畸变——但需要付出更多的努力。有趣的是，其中一片镜头从玻璃纸信封里拿出来时上面有一块污迹。用超细纤维布快速擦拭一下就很好地清洁了镜头，但这让我困惑——为什么会有污迹？<br /><br />广角镜头还配有一个微距镜头，用于近摄简直令人惊叹。我用这个镜头拍摄图案编织、表面特写、动物毛发和其他有趣的主题（我牛仔裤的膝盖处拍出来是非常有趣的背景）。它效果

**解释**：产品：镜头和滤镜套装｜声音：未提及声音｜其他：价格、与三星NX300的兼容性、长焦镜头、广角镜头、近摄镜、微距镜头、图像质量、畸变、污迹

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-100　G109

**原文**：Even though I have always had an interest in astronomy I was never able to work a course in this subject in my college schedule or afterwards.  When I saw this telescope offered I realized that now I had both the time and the means to explore my interest a bit further.  I had very little difficulty setting the scope up, and spent some time familiarizing myself with the various components.  I practiced using this by checking out objects in my neighborhood, and managed to get an 'up close and personal' view of some birds in a neighbor's yard and quickly realized that this scope was powerful.<br /><br />That evening I took the scope to an upstairs window to see what I could see, and despite the pesky street light was able to get a great view of the full moon (btw did you know that it sparkles a bit?).  I saw details that I had only seen in photos before which was fascinating.  Then I decided to take a look at Mars, which was near the Moon that evening.  That is when things got complicated.  The finder scope and the main scope need to be synced, and I don't know how to do this (yet).  With some fiddling, and aided by Mars' close proximity to the Moon I was able to find Mars, which was exciting but for some reason it was distorted (it had a starburst appearance).  My husband and I fiddled with various lenses and combinations to try to eliminate this but were unsuccessful.  The experimentation was further complicated by the touchiness of the stand....it was difficult to make small, smooth adjustments and to keep the scope locked in position.  Some of this is no doubt due to our in expertise, some I think is due to the equipment.<br /><br />My enjoyment of this scope would have been much greater if more instructions had been provided.  We discovered, more or less by accident how to lock, at least partially, the scope into place.  We managed to make some focusing adjustments by trial and error.  Even some rudimentary instructions in a small booklet or even a sheet or two describing how to adjust, focus and lock the scope as well as an illustration of the scope with all the components clearly labeled with a brief description of their function would have been greatly appreciated. This is a basic instrument, one that will primarily be used by beginners or novices who will not necessarily be familiar with this equipment.  Basic instructions should have been included.  As it is my unfamiliarity with the various aspects of the telescope make following many of the instructions I do find on-line difficult to follow.<br /><br />Despite this problems I am enjoying my new toy.  I found the stand to be steady, and the tray located on the stand to be a handy place to put lenses and covers.  As I mentioned earlier this is a surprisingly (at least to this beginning user) powerful scope.  I'm going to play around with this some more, and will probably upgrade some of the lenses and add some accessories based on my experiences.  Besides star gazing I think this will be a great item to watch birds or wild life, and it is certainly portable and forgiving enough to take on a camping trip.

**翻译**：尽管我一直对天文学感兴趣，但在大学期间或之后都没能修读这门课程。当我看到这款望远镜出售时，我意识到现在我有时间和手段来进一步探索我的兴趣了。我安装望远镜几乎没有遇到什么困难，并花了一些时间熟悉各个部件。我通过观察附近的东西来练习使用它，成功近距离看到了邻居院子里的几只鸟，很快意识到这款望远镜很强大。<br /><br />那天晚上，我把望远镜带到楼上的窗户前看看能观察到什么，尽管有讨厌的路灯，我还是能很好地看到满月（顺便说一下，你知道月亮有点闪闪发光吗？）。我看到了以前只在照片中见过的细节，这很迷人。然后我决定看看火星，那天晚上火星在月亮附近。这时事情变得复杂了。寻星镜和主镜需要同步，而我不知道怎么做（目前）。经过一番摆弄，借助火星靠近月亮的优势，我找到了火星，这很令人兴奋，但不知为何它变形了（呈现出星芒状外观）。我和丈夫摆弄了各种镜头和组合，试图消除这个问题，但没有成功。实验还因支架的敏感性而变得更加复杂……很难进行微小、平滑的调整，并保持望远镜锁定在位置上。其中一些无疑是由于我们的不熟练，一些我认为是由于设备本身。<br /><br />如果提供了更多的说明，我对这款望远镜的享受会大得多。我们或多或少是偶然发现了如何至少部分地将望远镜锁定到位。我们通过反复试验进行了一些对焦调整。即使是一本小册子中的一些基本说明，甚至一两张纸描述如何调整、对焦和锁定望远镜，以及一张清晰标注所有部件并简要描述其功能的望远镜插图，也会非常受欢迎。这是一个基本仪器，主要供初学者或新手使用，他们不一定熟悉这种设备。应该包含基本说明。由于我对望远镜各个方面的不熟悉，我很难遵循我在网上找到的许多说明。<br /><br />尽管有这些问题，我还是很享受我的新玩具。我发现支架很稳固，支架上的托盘是放置镜头和盖子的方便地方。正如我之前提到的，这是一个令人惊讶的（至少对这位初学者来说）强大的望远镜。我会继续摆弄它，并可能根据我的经验升级一些镜头和添加一些配件。除了观星，我想这将会是一个

**解释**：产品：望远镜｜声音：未提及声音｜其他：安装、使用、寻星镜与主镜同步、火星变形、支架调整、说明书不足、支架稳固、托盘方便、升级镜头和配件

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S1-078　G116

**原文**：After a bad experience with another flat amp antenna I purchased the leaf for just over $50 .<br />It picks up 19 stations in the "low country" around a lot of tall pine trees.(20 is the max number of stations in a 60 mile radius here with any antenna according to the TV manufacturer).<br /><br />You can paint the ant/ cables to match the walls. Comes with push pins for walls and Velcro for wall or window if you want them.Also has a plug into TV for the amp or you can plug into the wall like I did so it isn't hampered by the surge protector.<br /><br /> I get ABC, CBS,NBC,FOX,THIS,3 PBS stations, 3 Christian channels,a Spanish channel, WE,GET,MY,BOUNCE,YOU TOO, one music video channel and one other. The reception on all (but 2) is exceptionally strong with no blips in power even if you cannot put it in a window. I have mine hanging behind my curtain and most channels get 90-100% station strength almost constantly when 35%, even 30% will give you the station clear enough to watch a program with very few "blips" in reception.<br /><br />I am enjoying not adjusting the Rabbit ear antenna (when I watched certain channels) even though it worked *good* enough for the money.<br /><br />If you had another antenna before that only picked up 5-13 channels (for exp.) and you switch to a more powerful one like this, remember to unplug your TV a good minute before re- auto programming ( I forgot at first myself) as some TVs seem to "remember" how many it gets and stops at that number.<br /><br />No need for an ugly outside antenna with this one.<br /><br />When I use my hair dryer or turn on another "power draw", it doesnt get the interference the previous amplified antenna had, so probably uses less electricity, yet pulls in more stations.<br />I'm very happy with it. I get more channels than my basic cable had that I enjoy watching also.

**翻译**：在另一款平板天线体验不佳后，我以略高于50美元的价格购买了leaf。它在松树环绕的“低地”地区能收到19个频道。（据电视制造商称，在60英里半径内，任何天线最多能收到20个频道。）你可以把天线和线缆涂成与墙壁匹配的颜色。附带用于墙壁的图钉和用于墙壁或窗户的魔术贴。还有一个插入电视的放大器插头，或者像我做的那样插到墙上，这样就不会受到电涌保护器的干扰。我能收到ABC、CBS、NBC、FOX、THIS、3个PBS频道、3个基督教频道、一个西班牙语频道、WE、GET、MY、BOUNCE、YOU TOO、一个音乐视频频道和另一个频道。除了2个频道外，所有频道的接收信号都异常强，即使不能放在窗户里，电力也没有中断。我把天线挂在窗帘后面，大多数频道的信号强度几乎一直保持在90-100%，而35%甚至30%就能让你清晰观看节目，接收几乎没有“中断”。我很享受不用调整兔耳天线（当我看某些频道时），尽管它对于这个价格来说已经足够好了。如果你之前有另一个天线只能收到5-13个频道（例如），然后换到像这样更强大的天线，记得在重新自动编程前拔掉电视插头至少一分钟（我自己一开始也忘了），因为有些电视似乎会“记住”它能收到多少频道并停在那里。有了这个天线，就不需要丑陋的室外天线了。当我使用吹风机或打开另一个“耗电设备”时，它不会像之前的放大天线那样受到干扰，所以可能耗电更少，却能收到更多频道。我对它非常满意。我收到的频道比我基本有线电视的还多，而且我也喜欢看。

**解释**：产品：平板电视天线｜声音：接收信号异常强，大多数频道信号强度几乎一直保持在90-100%，35%甚至30%就能清晰观看，接收几乎没有“中断”；使用吹风机或打开其他耗电设备时不会受到干扰。｜其他：价格略高于50美元；可涂漆匹配墙壁；附带图钉和魔术贴；可插入电视或墙壁；收到多个频道；无需调整兔耳天线；重新自动编程前需拔掉电视插头一分钟；不需要室外天线；比基本有线电视收到更多频道。

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

**原文**：If you are new to sound systems, this is a good way to start.
