## S2-069　G118

**原文**：I had the original stick and like most technology I noticed it glitching and freezing and restarting more and more! It was maddening so I finally snagged a new version and I’m so glad I did! Not only can I now tell it to open my ring front porch camera but I can now control volume and turn my tv on and off with the remote! This was so worth the upgrade and what do you know!......freezing and glitching is gone now!

**翻译**：我有原来的那款棒状设备，和大多数科技产品一样，我注意到它越来越频繁地出现故障、卡顿和重启！这让人抓狂，所以我终于入手了新版本，我很高兴我这么做了！现在我不但可以叫它打开我的Ring前门摄像头，还可以用遥控器控制音量、开关电视！这次升级太值了，而且你猜怎么着！……卡顿和故障现在都消失了！

**解释**：产品：流媒体棒/电视棒设备（可能为Fire TV Stick）｜声音：未提及声音相关描述｜其他：提及原版设备出现故障、卡顿和重启；新版可打开Ring前门摄像头、控制音量、开关电视；认为升级值得；新版不再卡顿和故障

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-041　G119

**原文**：Have both the 10x42 and their 12x42 binoteck brand binos. This review is for the 10x42. For the price point, these are very good binos for reqular use between me any my kids. TBH, when compared to their 12x42, they seems identical. The 10x42 is slightly less than 10 power, but extremely close, compared to my other name brand binos.<br /><br />Kids are hard on binos (dropping, touchy, etc.) and they have held up well in the past week we have used them. The texture on the binos have a solid grip and it would be hard to have them slip out of your hands, even with gloves on.  Compared to more expensive binos, I am very impressed by this product.<br /><br />I find that prism binos are much easier on my eyes and these fit the bill. For well lighted conditions, the images are crisp and clear. These are ok for lower light conditions but if that is important to you, then you are easily going to be paying LOTS more for a named-brand binos to get the extra clarity/crispness needed in those conditions.  This was a balancing act and it worked for my purposes. I can keep a pair in the truck and not worry about them being stolen.

**翻译**：我有10x42和他们的12x42 binoteck品牌的望远镜。这篇评论是关于10x42的。就价格而言，这些是非常好的望远镜，适合我和孩子们日常使用。说实话，与他们的12x42相比，它们看起来一模一样。与我的其他名牌望远镜相比，10x42的倍率略低于10倍，但非常接近。<br /><br />孩子们对望远镜很粗鲁（掉落、触摸等），在我们使用的一周里，它们保持得很好。望远镜的纹理有坚实的抓地力，即使戴着手套也很难从手中滑落。与更昂贵的望远镜相比，我对这个产品印象深刻。<br /><br />我发现棱镜望远镜对我的眼睛更友好，这些符合要求。在光线充足的条件下，图像清晰锐利。这些在低光条件下还可以，但如果这对你很重要，那么你很容易要花更多的钱购买名牌望远镜，以获得那些条件下所需的额外清晰度/锐度。这是一个平衡行为，它符合我的目的。我可以把一副放在卡车里，不用担心被偷。

**解释**：产品：望远镜｜声音：在光线充足的条件下，图像清晰锐利（crisp and clear）；在低光条件下还可以（ok for lower light conditions），但需要花更多钱购买名牌望远镜以获得额外清晰度/锐度（extra clarity/crispness）。｜其他：价格点（price point）、适合日常使用（regular use）、与12x42比较（compared to their 12x42）、倍率略低于10倍（slightly less than 10 power）、耐用性（held up well）、纹理抓地力（solid grip）、棱镜设计对眼睛友好（prism binos easier on eyes）、平衡行为（balancing act）、可放在卡车里不怕被偷（keep in truck not worry about stolen）。

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-054　G120

**原文**：The primary purpose of this speaker is to have something louder than my phone in the shower, around the pool, or at the beach. For the price, it is a good solution.<br /><br />Battery life and portability appear excellent.  Very easy to connect and it can get fairly loud without significant distortion. In terms of tonal quality it’s not bad. Very mid-range heavy. I typically find myself using an in-app EQ to boost the highs to get more clarity. It’s a small speaker so don’t expect any bass.  At the beach with constant ambient noise, this works.<br /><br />Biggest drawback is poor Bluetooth reception. 10-15ft and it starts dropping out badly and it’s even worse if your body is in between the phone and the speaker. I don’t have a big pool but i can’t be on one side with my iPhone 11Pro and have a clear, consistent connection to the speaker. If Bluetooth reception were stronger, this would be 5-stars for value. But again, for having a little music while sitting with a friend at the beach, it works well enough.<br /><br />For the price, I’d recommend it as a good value.

**翻译**：这个扬声器的主要用途是在淋浴、泳池边或海滩上，有一个比手机更响亮的设备。就价格而言，这是一个不错的解决方案。<br /><br />电池续航和便携性似乎非常出色。连接非常容易，而且可以相当响亮而没有明显失真。在音质方面还不错。中频非常突出。我通常会用应用内的均衡器来提升高频以获得更多清晰度。这是一个小扬声器，所以不要期望有任何低音。在海滩上持续的环境噪音中，这个可以派上用场。<br /><br />最大的缺点是蓝牙接收差。10-15英尺就开始严重断连，如果身体挡在手机和扬声器之间就更糟了。我没有大泳池，但我不能带着iPhone 11Pro在一侧与扬声器保持清晰、稳定的连接。如果蓝牙接收更强，就性价比而言这会是5星。但再次强调，在海滩上和朋友坐着听点音乐，它足够好用。<br /><br />就价格而言，我会推荐它，性价比不错。

**解释**：产品：便携式蓝牙扬声器｜声音：可以相当响亮而没有明显失真（fairly loud without significant distortion），音质还不错（not bad），中频非常突出（Very mid-range heavy），通常用应用内均衡器提升高频以获得更多清晰度（boost the highs to get more clarity），小扬声器不要期望低音（don't expect any bass）｜其他：主要用途是在淋浴、泳池边或海滩上比手机更响亮；电池续航和便携性似乎非常出色；连接非常容易；最大缺点是蓝牙接收差，10-15英尺开始严重断连，身体阻挡时更糟；就价格而言推荐，性价比不错

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

Also I find it big for my living room and the sound could be better, for the price I paid.<br />The base is so loud I only use it when company comes over.<br />I actually thought I could hear the YV in other rooms of my house with the sound bar but 🙁 not🙁<br />Also cannot connect my iPad Pro my iphoneX to the sound bar

## S2-002　G131

**原文**：The Logitech Folio Touch is a JOY to type on. I really love typing on it — I’m writing this review using the case. I’ve typed quite a few long documents on it. I turn to this keyboard when I want distraction-free writing.<br /><br />The keyboard reminds me of Apple laptop keyboards before the disastrous butterfly keyboards. The keyboard is backlit, and the brightness of the backlighting can be adjusted via the function keys. The row of function keys is another good feature for me. I find it much faster and easier to adjust the iPad’s brightness or volume using the function keys.<br /><br />I downloaded the Logitech Control app, but it doesn’t seem to give any options for customizing the keyboard. All I see the app do is check for keyboard firmware updates. When I wanted to change the trackpad scrolling direction, I had to hunt through the iPad’s settings — I thought that would be in the Logitech app. (I learned those settings became part of iOS, so the Logitech app seems … unnecessary.)<br /><br />I don’t really like the trackpad. I’ve used Logitech mice for years. My current daily mouse is the Logitech MX Master 3, so I *know* they can do a good Mac mouse. But I just feel like they dropped the ball with this trackpad. The trackpad works but it could be better. I notice drag at times, and I don’t like the noise or tactile feedback when I click it. I can adjust a few settings (scrolling direction, tracking speed, tap to click, and two finger secondary click) in the iPad’s General Settings, but I want to be able to customize more through Logitech.<br /><br />The corners of the case are reinforced. The bumper is a bit thick, too thick for some hubs or USB-C cables to fit without using an extension cable. There is a cutout for the power button so Touch ID can be used. There’s a spot for the Apple Pencil, it can rest inside the bumper or fit through a slot on the latch.<br /><br />The Folio Touch pairs via the Smart Connector, so it’s super easy to set up: just stick your iPad inside and it’s ready to go. The iPad powers the keyboard, so I don’t have to remember to charge it, which I like. I haven’t noticed it being a battery hog.<br /><br />Overall, I really like the Logitech Folio Touch. I actually use my iPad more now, since I like typing on the keyboard so much. The case has made my iPad more functional for me.

**翻译**：罗技Folio Touch打字是一种享受。我真的很喜欢在上面打字——这篇评测就是用这个保护套写的。我已经在上面打了相当多的长文档。当我想进行无干扰写作时，我就会用这个键盘。<br /><br />这个键盘让我想起苹果笔记本电脑在灾难性的蝴蝶键盘之前的键盘。键盘有背光，背光亮度可以通过功能键调节。功能键那一行对我来说是另一个好功能。我发现用功能键调节iPad的亮度或音量要快得多、容易得多。<br /><br />我下载了Logitech Control应用，但它似乎没有提供任何自定义键盘的选项。我看到这个应用所做的只是检查键盘固件更新。当我想改变触控板的滚动方向时，我不得不在iPad的设置里翻找——我以为那会在罗技应用里。（我了解到那些设置已经成为iOS的一部分，所以罗技应用似乎……没必要。）<br /><br />我不太喜欢这个触控板。我用罗技鼠标很多年了。我目前日常用的鼠标是罗技MX Master 3，所以我*知道*他们能做出好的Mac鼠标。但我觉得他们在这个触控板上失手了。触控板能用，但可以更好。我有时注意到拖拽，而且我不喜欢点击时的噪音或触觉反馈。我可以在iPad的通用设置里调整一些设置（滚动方向、跟踪速度、轻点点击和双指辅助点击），但我希望能通过罗技自定义更多。<br /><br />保护套的边角是加固的。缓冲边框有点厚，对于某些集线器或USB-C线缆来说太厚了，不用延长线就插不进去。电源按钮有一个开口，所以可以使用Touch ID。有一个放Apple Pencil的位置，它可以放在缓冲边框内，也可以穿过锁扣上的一个槽。<br /><br />Folio Touch通过Smart Connector配对，所以设置超级简单：只要把iPad放进去就可以用了。iPad给键盘供电，所以我不必记得给它充电，这点我喜欢。我没有注意到它很耗电。<br /><br />总的来说，我真的很喜欢罗技Folio Touch。实际上我现在用iPad更多了，因为我太喜欢在这个键盘上打字了。这个保护套让我的iPad对我来说更有用。

**解释**：产品：罗技Folio Touch iPad键盘保护套｜声音：未提及声音。｜其他：打字体验、背光、功能键、Logitech Control应用、触控板、保护套设计、Smart Connector配对、供电、整体使用。

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S1-008　G132

## S2-023　G137

**原文**：I'm a big fan of the 8x32 style of binoculars. These aren't as bright or powerful as the 10x42 or 8x42 binoculars on the market, but they are 1/3 to 1/2 less bulky and lighter weight. I'm more of a hiker than a birder, so when I'm hiking 20 miles, the extra weight and space in my backpack is very noticeable. Realistically, the larger 42mm binoculars are mostly brighter than the 32mm size at dawn and dusk, which is not when I'm hiking. 8x vs 10x is a personal preference, but a lot of people prefer 8x because the image is easier to hand-hold (in addition to the smaller size and lower weight).<br /><br />Celestron makes a wide variety of binoculars, including a lot of basic-quality entry-level stuff that is not built very well and doesn't perform very well. This TrailSeeker ED model is a big step up from entry-level, both in price and performance. This model isn't quite as good as the top-rated 8x32 models like Nikon Monarch or Zeiss Terra, which both sell for around $400, but I think it is at least as good as any of the competition selling for under $300 that I have tried. I see a bright clear image with no noticeable chromatic aberration. Field of view is much better than most 8x32 binoculars and the image is sharp over a wider area than other under-$300 binoculars that I have tried. Field of view is very useful if your subject moves quickly or unpredictably (birds, wild mammals, sports athletes, etc). Build quality has been very solid and reliable for me so far.<br /><br />Some people complain that the Celestron warranty is not as good as the competition and this is true. Some of the other brands have a "no questions asked" return policy for a good period time. Celestron's warranty is very limited in comparison, so definitely take that into consideration, especially if you are hard on your equipment.

**翻译**：我非常喜欢8x32规格的双筒望远镜。它们不如市场上的10x42或8x42双筒望远镜那么明亮或强大，但体积和重量要少三分之一到一半。我更像是一名徒步旅行者，而不是观鸟者，所以当我徒步20英里时，背包中额外的重量和空间非常明显。实际上，较大的42毫米双筒望远镜在黎明和黄昏时大多比32毫米尺寸更明亮，而那时我并不是在徒步。8倍与10倍是个人偏好，但很多人更喜欢8倍，因为图像更容易手持（除了更小的尺寸和更轻的重量）。<br /><br />星特朗生产各种各样的双筒望远镜，包括许多基本质量的入门级产品，这些产品做工不太好，性能也不太好。这款TrailSeeker ED型号在价格和性能上都比入门级有了很大的提升。这款型号不如尼康Monarch或蔡司Terra等顶级8x32型号那么好，它们的售价都在400美元左右，但我认为它至少和我试过的任何300美元以下的竞争对手一样好。我看到明亮清晰的图像，没有明显的色差。视野比大多数8x32双筒望远镜好得多，而且在我试过的其他300美元以下的双筒望远镜中，图像在更宽的区域内保持清晰。如果你的拍摄对象移动迅速或不可预测（鸟类、野生哺乳动物、运动员等），视野非常有用。到目前为止，做工质量对我来说非常扎实可靠。<br /><br />有些人抱怨星特朗的保修不如竞争对手，这是事实。其他一些品牌在相当长的时间内有无条件退货政策。相比之下，星特朗的保修非常有限，所以一定要考虑到这一点，特别是如果你对设备很粗暴的话。

**解释**：产品：不确定｜声音：（仅翻译，无解析：该条反复触发中性闸门，按兜底策略只提供翻译）｜其他：

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-051　G138

**原文**：First some reviewers have said that it is plastic mount. The bottom of the lens is plastic but the mount itself where it touches the camera is blackend aluminum.<br />I have used this lens on a Nikon D200, D300 and currently on a Nikon D7100.<br />It has the field of view of about 45-450 mm lens on the DX cameras.<br />I  have been using this lens for 5-6 years. It is reported that the AF is slow, well I have made bird photos with the D300. I have used it in very dusty conditions.<br />Lab tests show that this lens has some distortion and is sharp enough for digital displays and 8x10 prints.<br />I have used this mainly for landscape and close ups, I am pleased with the results.<br />All in all, it's a good, cheap lens.

**翻译**：首先，一些评论者说它是塑料卡口。镜头底部是塑料的，但接触相机的卡口本身是发黑铝制的。<br />我在尼康D200、D300上使用过这款镜头，目前用在尼康D7100上。<br />在DX相机上，它的视场角大约相当于45-450毫米镜头。<br />我使用这款镜头已经5-6年了。据说自动对焦很慢，嗯，我用D300拍过鸟类照片。我在非常多尘的条件下使用过它。<br />实验室测试显示这款镜头有一些畸变，对于数字显示和8x10打印来说足够锐利。<br />我主要用它拍摄风景和特写，对结果很满意。<br />总而言之，这是一款不错、便宜的镜头。

**解释**：产品：镜头｜声音：未提及声音相关内容｜其他：卡口材质（塑料底部、发黑铝制卡口）、兼容相机（尼康D200、D300、D7100）、视场角（DX相机上约45-450毫米）、使用时长（5-6年）、自动对焦速度（据说很慢，但用D300拍过鸟类照片）、使用环境（非常多尘）、实验室测试（有畸变，对数字显示和8x10打印足够锐利）、拍摄用途（风景和特写）、总体评价（不错、便宜的镜头）

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S3-085　G145

**原文**：I got this because I needed a basic router that could handle multiple connections and not break under the strain.  I needed all 4 ports for a desktop (gigabit Ethernet destroys wireless for ping on multiplayer games!), PS3 (same reason), and two networked printers.  I also have three or four devices that connect wirelessly.  This router was easy to set up, had good security options and seemed to work fine for a couple of months.  But I found that YouTube on the wireless connection with a Windows Update running means that my Android smartphone net speed slows to a crawl.  I pay for a 20mbps connection and Speedtest shows that this isn't giving it to me.<br /><br />This router cannot handle multiple connections very well.  Downloading large open-source code packages via P2P networks chokes the pipe to a crawl OR kills it altogether until an unplug and restart.  I have a 1000 square foot apartment with this in one bedroom and when I'm on the other side of the apartment, I don't get full signal.<br /><br />This is a good basic router for a single user who primarily streams YouTube or Netflix to one laptop at a time.  If you are a power user or really need good range, then you need to spend more money on a N600 or N900-level router.  Additionally, since this router does not specify compatibility with IPv6, a more expensive router might be more future proof.<br /><br />I never used the USB port so I can't comment on it.  I never flashed Tomato to it, so I am not sure if that it a good thing or not.  I just know that this router is not really optimized to take advantage of superfast modem speeds.

**翻译**：我买这个是因为我需要一个基本的路由器，能够处理多个连接并且在压力下不会崩溃。我需要所有4个端口用于台式机（千兆以太网在多人游戏中的延迟方面完胜无线！）、PS3（同样的原因）和两台网络打印机。我还有三四个无线连接的设备。这个路由器设置简单，有良好的安全选项，并且似乎正常工作了几个月。但我发现，在Windows更新运行时，通过无线连接看YouTube会导致我的安卓智能手机网速慢如蜗牛。我支付的是20mbps的连接，但Speedtest显示它并没有给我这个速度。<br /><br />这个路由器不能很好地处理多个连接。通过P2P网络下载大型开源代码包会使管道堵塞到爬行速度，或者完全断掉，直到拔掉插头重启。我有一个1000平方英尺的公寓，把这个放在一间卧室里，当我在公寓的另一边时，我得不到完整的信号。<br /><br />对于主要一次向一台笔记本电脑流式传输YouTube或Netflix的单个用户来说，这是一个不错的基本路由器。如果你是高级用户或者真的需要良好的覆盖范围，那么你需要花更多的钱买N600或N900级别的路由器。此外，由于这个路由器没有指定与IPv6的兼容性，更昂贵的路由器可能更具未来适应性。<br /><br />我从未使用过USB端口，所以我无法评论它。我从未刷过Tomato固件，所以我不确定这是好事还是坏事。我只知道这个路由器并没有真正优化以利用超快的调制解调器速度。

**解释**：产品：路由器｜声音：未提及声音｜其他：多个连接处理不佳；YouTube流媒体和Windows更新时网速下降；P2P下载导致管道堵塞或断掉；1000平方英尺公寓内信号覆盖不全；适合单用户流媒体；建议高级用户购买N600或N900级别；未指定IPv6兼容性；未使用USB端口；未刷Tomato固件；未优化以利用超快调制解调器速度。

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S3-082　G146

**原文**：edit: 7/3/2020<br />5 months in and it just died. Was sitting on my desk, I shut it off one weekend, came home 3 days later and nothing. It had power but no boot. Ended up sending it in to Lenovo for repair and they are claiming out of warranty due to spill damage which is totally wrong. I contacted Amazon and they are letting me return the item directly to amazon minus a restock fee. I guarantee there was no spill damage and I use to do tech support for HP, that was a common line agents tried as it was their job to deny as many repairs as possible. So I can no longer recommend this PC or Lenovo in general. I had a bad experience with their Rift S with oculus as well. Just not built to last. and support is obviously borderline scam. Stay away from Lenovo.  far away. It also took them 3 months to ship me a stupid box only to try pulling this scam on me. And guess how much they wanted to repair it. 486 bucks!!! I only paid 430...  lol  What a joke!  but Amazon support as always is amazing!<br /><br />old post:<br />I have had flip laptops like this since 2002 in college. They test piloted this feature at my campus.  I have been addicted to this type of laptop since and haven't had one for a while.... I swapped the 4 gigs of replaceable ram out for a 16gb sodimm giving this laptop 20gb of ram. I wouldn't recomend doing yourself unless you know what you are doing. Removing the RAM cover was moderately difficult without proper tools. I used the plastic piece from the ram where it hangs on a hook to pop the clips open. But this is a laptop, a tablet, a tent, it's just great so far... It's small and packs a lot of power. Anyone complaining about battery life doesn't understand this thing is powerful.... It's comparable with my 2 desktops which are a ryzen 2700x and 2600x both water cooled. so... That is quite a statement to make comparing the two with one another. The only thing I don't like is the USB ports are very very tight, I am afraid I will break them unplugging things they are so tight... But I am hoping they break in a bit, otherwise I may look for a short extension cord to keep plugged in permanently. or maybe a hub that has a looser plug that fits this thing better. I know that will break eventually with such tight connection... I have seen it before so just be careful... EVen without upgrading the memory this thing is great. It was on sale for 595 bucks new or 500 used. I got it used (knowing it couldn't really be used, which it wasn't, just damaged box) and I had a 60 dollar credit from getting the amazon card. So the whole thing with memory cost about 500 even. I order the Corsair 16gb SODIMM from Amazon btw,  I wasn't sure which sodimm to get, but I trust Corsair and I chose wisely I think. works great.  The fingerprint login button is also amazing. which I find it funny I am saying that since that is not something I would really consider getting given the choice.... The Vega 10 also is amazing.  this plays World of Warcraft at low settings perfectly fine, 30-45 fps, I didn't try a raid yet but dungeons are no problem. but raids will likely be a bit laggy, but I'm just speaking from past experience with other machines. Diablo 3 worked great, and I hook my Oculus Go up to it via VR Desktop.  It works amazingly on that end and I would recommend this machine just for doing wireless VR with the Go/Quest..  Assuming it keeps working for me into the future, I made a good buy on this one. Been eyeing up a new laptop for several months now.  I would get this one if I were you. I have 2 computer science degree and run an AI startup company. So I think my advice carries some slight weight :) lol<br /><br />Upgraded RAM or not, good machine.  Only thing I haven't been able to adjust yet is the dedicated video memory being used. The normal bios tricks aren't working and since I have 20 gb ram, i want 4 dedicated to video and not 2.gigs as default. so If anyone knows the proper method for this machine, let me know!  I'll prolly figure it myself here though in the next coming days...<br /><br />Again, buy this laptop. I swore I would never buy another lenovo product after buying the Oculus Rift S (since it was produced by lenovo and oculus in partnership) But I feel I was wrong to make such a harsh judgement after using this machine and also after dismantling it and looking it over....  It won't survive any extreme use, so treat it well. but how could it with such a small form factor..<br /><br />The only issue I have noticed is with the wifi as I have read from other users as well. I have had the wifi board crash one time already. solution was to disable wifi and re-enable. trying to reconnect would fail until you do this.... So, that is somewhat annoying if it happens with frequency....  I will update on this issue as time continue....<br /><br />also on the wifi note, it can connect to wifi and share the wifi to other machines via hotspot with no difficulty, I tethered my oculus Go to it this way. nice... some lan cards can't do this well...<br /><br />edit: had several more wifi drops. i did a BIOS update with the lenovo app, and changed the power settings in power options for wifi to best performance, it was set to a strange sleep type low power setting. I'm not sure if it was the power options or the bios update but all is working well so far now, knock on wood...

**翻译**：编辑：2020年7月3日<br />用了5个月就坏了。它当时放在我的桌子上，我周末把它关掉，3天后回来就什么反应都没有了。它有电但无法启动。最后我把它寄给联想维修，他们声称由于液体损坏而超出保修期，这完全是错误的。我联系了亚马逊，他们允许我直接退货给亚马逊，但扣除重新上架费。我保证没有液体损坏，我以前在惠普做过技术支持，这是客服人员常用的一种说辞，因为他们的工作就是尽可能拒绝维修。所以我不再推荐这台电脑或联想整体。我在他们的Rift S与Oculus上也有过糟糕的经历。就是不耐用。而且支持服务显然近乎诈骗。远离联想。远远地。他们还花了3个月才给我寄来一个破盒子，然后试图对我进行这种诈骗。猜猜他们想收多少维修费。486美元！！！我只花了430... 哈哈 真是个笑话！但亚马逊的支持一如既往地棒！<br /><br />旧帖：<br />我从2002年大学时就开始用这种翻转笔记本电脑。他们在我校园里试点了这个功能。从那以后我就迷上了这种类型的笔记本电脑，已经有一段时间没有用了... 我把4GB的可更换内存换成了16GB的SO-DIMM，使这台笔记本电脑有20GB的内存。除非你知道自己在做什么，否则我不建议自己动手。没有合适的工具，拆下内存盖有点困难。我用内存条上挂在钩子上的塑料片撬开了卡扣。但这是一台笔记本电脑、平板电脑、帐篷模式，到目前为止就是很棒... 它很小但功能强大。任何抱怨电池续航的人都不明白这东西很强大... 它可以与我的两台台式机相媲美，分别是Ryzen 2700X和2600X，都是水冷的。所以... 将两者进行比较，这是一个相当了不起的声明。唯一不喜欢的是USB端口非常非常紧，我担心拔插东西时会弄坏它们，它们太紧了... 但我希望它们能稍微松动一些，否则我可能会找一个短延长线永久插着。或者找一个插头更松的集线器，更适合这个东西。我知道这么紧的连接最终会坏... 我以前见过，所以要小心... 即使不升级内存，这东西也很棒。它新品促销价595美元，二手500美元。我买了二手的（知道它实际上几乎没用过，确实没用过，只是盒子损坏），而且我还有60美元的亚马逊信用卡返现。所以加上内存总共花了大约500美元。顺便说一句，我从亚马逊订购了Corsair 16GB SO-DIMM，我不确定该买哪个SO-DIMM，但我信任Corsair，我认为我选对了。运行良好。指纹登录按钮也很棒。我觉得这很有趣，因为如果让我选择，我通常不会考虑这个功能... Vega 10也很棒。这可以在低设置下玩《魔兽世界》

**解释**：产品：不确定｜声音：（仅翻译，无解析：该条反复触发中性闸门，按兜底策略只提供翻译）｜其他：

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-065　G157

**原文**：If you want a 10" tablet for reading books and watching videos/TV shows/movies and can't afford more money than this (price regularly drops to $100), then this is good product choice. Performance is fine for e-books and videos, but sometimes sluggish for intense game playing, photo/video editing or even text editing. The SD card slot lets you store lots of TV shows and movies and use the main memory for apps (which often work poorly if stored on the SD card). Kindle e-books sometimes work much better on the Fire tablets than on ipads or Android tablets, especially if the books have embedded audio or video. The user interface revolves around the books and videos that you bought from Amazon. With a considerable amount of work, you may be able to replace the user interface with a more standard Android launcher that focuses on your apps and files. Realistically, the performance of this tablet clunky enough that I don't recommend it unless you are mostly using it for e-books and videos, so maybe changing the user interface is not worth the effort.<br /><br />You should also ask yourself if you are using the tablet for mostly e-books and videos, do you really need a 10 inch screen? The 8 inch Kindle Fire tablet has similar performance and a much lower price and is also lighter weight and less bulky, making it easier to use in many situations.

**翻译**：如果你想要一台10英寸的平板电脑来阅读书籍和观看视频/电视节目/电影，并且无法承担比这更多的钱（价格通常会降到100美元），那么这是一个不错的产品选择。对于电子书和视频来说，性能还可以，但在玩大型游戏、照片/视频编辑甚至文本编辑时有时会卡顿。SD卡槽让你可以存储大量的电视节目和电影，并将主内存用于应用程序（如果存储在SD卡上，应用程序通常运行不佳）。Kindle电子书在Fire平板电脑上有时比在iPad或Android平板电脑上运行得好得多，特别是如果书籍内嵌有音频或视频。用户界面主要围绕你从亚马逊购买的书籍和视频。经过大量工作，你或许可以将用户界面替换为更标准的Android启动器，专注于你的应用和文件。实际上，这款平板电脑的性能足够笨重，除非你主要用它来看电子书和视频，否则我不推荐它，所以也许更改用户界面不值得费力。你还应该问自己，如果你主要用平板电脑看电子书和视频，你真的需要10英寸的屏幕吗？8英寸的Kindle Fire平板电脑具有相似的性能，价格低得多，而且更轻便、更不笨重，在许多情况下更容易使用。

**解释**：产品：10英寸平板电脑（Kindle Fire）｜声音：未提及｜其他：性能、SD卡槽、Kindle电子书、用户界面、8英寸Kindle Fire平板电脑

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-085　G162

**原文**：I needed an inexpensive DVD player to use in the basement, but still wanted to have good picture quality. I gave this a shot (bought it used) and have been more than pleased with the results.<br /><br />Setup was a snap if you have an HDMI port (basically just plug in the HDMI cable, plug in the power cord, and you're done!). The on-screen setup menus were decent. Lots of options to choose from including several picture quality choices. The manual sort of explains the options, but some "lets see what this does" is helpful as well, especially with the picture quality options.<br /><br />The player feels a bit "tinny' but that's pretty much par for the course for low-ended DVD players. The remote is decent - the buttons could be bigger, but it's acceptable. No eject button on the remote, however, which was an unexpected omission.<br /><br />The picture quality is excellent - rivals a much more expensive Oppo DVD. Much, much better than I was expecting at this price point.<br /><br />I've tried to unlock this to play region 2 disks but have had no luck. Tried a couple of options listed on AV discussion boards, but so far nothing. If anyone has had success unlocking this to play something other than Region 1 discs, I would love to hear how you did it.<br /><br />Overall, a great DVD player, and a good value even at the regular price. Highly recommend.

**翻译**：我需要一台便宜的DVD播放机放在地下室使用，但仍然希望有好的画质。我尝试了这台（买的二手），对结果非常满意。<br /><br />如果你有HDMI接口，设置非常简单（基本上就是插上HDMI线，插上电源线，就完成了！）。屏幕上的设置菜单还不错。有很多选项可供选择，包括几种画质选择。手册有点解释了这些选项，但有些“看看这个有什么用”的尝试也很有帮助，尤其是在画质选项方面。<br /><br />播放机感觉有点“单薄”，但这对于低端DVD播放机来说基本上是正常的。遥控器还不错——按钮可以更大一些，但可以接受。然而，遥控器上没有弹出按钮，这是一个意外的遗漏。<br /><br />画质非常出色——可以媲美贵得多的Oppo DVD。比我在这个价位上预期的要好得多。<br /><br />我试图解锁这台机器以播放2区光盘，但没有成功。尝试了AV讨论板上列出的几种方法，但到目前为止都不行。如果有人成功解锁这台机器以播放非1区光盘，我很想知道你是怎么做到的。<br /><br />总的来说，这是一台很棒的DVD播放机，即使按正常价格来说也是物有所值。强烈推荐。

**解释**：产品：DVD播放机｜声音：评论者没有提到声音。｜其他：画质、设置、遥控器、区域解锁、性价比

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-022　G165

**原文**：I have a Noctua NH-D15 in another case that I use as a workstation. When I was building a Ryzen x570 with a 3700x system I wanted to install something that was more manageable and was more sturdy (I had to ship the end result).<br /><br />Heat and noise<br /><br />I was concerned that the end result might be louder, but it ended up working rather well. I settled on a slightly higher idle temperature of 45-50c for the CPU to keep the fans from revving up and down. In the end the issues I had with my build were not with the CPU but rather with the south bridge of the x570 instead.<br /><br />Clearance<br /><br />I had no issue with clearance with the board and ram.<br /><br />Sturdiness<br /><br />There was no damage to the heat sink. I shipped with fedex in a double box in a fractal define C case.

**翻译**：我在另一个机箱里有一个Noctua NH-D15，用作工作站。当我组装一台搭载3700x的Ryzen x570系统时，我想安装一个更易于管理且更坚固的东西（我不得不将最终成品寄送出去）。<br /><br />热量和噪音<br /><br />我担心最终成品可能会更吵，但结果它运行得相当好。我选择了略高的CPU空闲温度45-50°C，以避免风扇转速忽高忽低。最终，我组装中遇到的问题不在CPU，而是在x570的南桥。<br /><br />间隙<br /><br />我在主板和内存的间隙方面没有问题。<br /><br />坚固性<br /><br />散热器没有损坏。我用联邦快递寄送，采用双层包装箱，装在Fractal Define C机箱里。

**解释**：产品：CPU散热器｜声音：担心最终成品可能会更吵（louder），但结果运行得相当好（working rather well），选择略高的CPU空闲温度45-50°C以避免风扇转速忽高忽低（keep the fans from revving up and down）｜其他：热量和噪音、间隙（clearance）、坚固性（sturdiness）、寄送（shipping）、Noctua NH-D15、Ryzen x570、3700x、南桥问题、主板和内存间隙、联邦快递、双层包装箱、Fractal Define C机箱

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S3-012　G179

**原文**：My wife loves birdwatching and our teenage son enjoys stargazing so we have an assortment of binoculars and inexpensive telescopes. Before this monocular I had never used one and even though I have not had it very long at all I am already very pleased with it. Though it is ideal for bird watching and field work, it also will work as an amateur telescope.<br /><br />At roughly 7&#34; long, this monocular is large enough to be usable but small enough to fit in a coat pocket or even in a briefcase. It comes in a nice, nylon-weave carrying pouch that even has a belt loop so carrying it into the field is easy and does not take up extra room. It also has a holding strap much like a camcorder does so it can be held more securely. It has a small metal tripod suitable for setting up on a desk or table. It’s not a big tripod - the legs extend to 7.5&#34; - but is works well if you are recording or are seated and need a steady hand. Finally, it includes a microfiber cleaning cloth in the box for wiping the lenses safely without scratching them.<br /><br />This is a waterproof monocular and everything about it showcases that. There are two rubberized caps for either end that cover the lenses and keep out moisture and dust when not in use. These are physically attached to the barrel (one by a rubber flap and one by a nylon cord) so there is no issue with losing the caps. Most of the barrel is rubberized as well except for the focus knob assembly and where it attaches.<br /><br />I used it to focus on the tree line behind the house (roughly 30 yards away) and was very pleased with how sharp and clear I was able to get the image. From the front of the house I was able to focus on the stop sign at the end of the street and had no issue with getting fine edge detail even at a distance of roughly a football field. The focus is very smooth and easy to adjust and getting the image in perfect focus is not the least bit difficult.<br /><br />Though I enjoy cameras and optical equipment and I have learned a good bit about optical equipment I am a far cry from being an expert, so I took this monocular to work and approached two teammates who are very well-versed in optics, photography and astronomy and I asked them to look it over and give me their honest opinion. Both of them examined it and expressed their approval. They both said similar things about it: the image through this monocular is very bright and clear, the focus is very smooth and it seems to be very well made. They both made a comment about the lens and that it was very good due to not having a darkened coating.<br /><br />One of my teammates told me to try looking at the stars with it. I had not really considered this but knew this would be an excellent test for it so I went outside after dark and pointed it skyward. Though I can definitely see more detail through a dedicated telescope, I was able to make out some of the larger surface detail on the moon including the high point in the middle of a crater. I was hindered by the fact that it is a full moon right now so I will have to try again this weekend during the eclipse. I then picked out a planet and looked at it through the monocular. Though it is a little tough to make out, I know I found Jupiter as I could see three of the moons as bright spots not visible to the naked eye (I have looked at it through a telescope so I recognize it from seeing it already). Finally I discovered a cluster of stars I could not see without magnification. This monocular is certainly not as good as a dedicated telescope but it is sufficient when camping and it eliminates the need to pack a big, bulky telescope unless you want to see more detail.<br /><br />This is a great tool for use in the field. It has very good range, the image is bright and clear and it even is sufficient for stargazing should you choose to use it for everything on the road. Great product!<br /><br />Disclaimer: I was provided a sample of this product in exchange for a fair, honest and thorough review. Even though I received this product at no cost I have rated and reviewed this product without bias, based solely on my experience with it.

**翻译**：我的妻子喜欢观鸟，我们十几岁的儿子喜欢观星，所以我们有各种各样的双筒望远镜和便宜的望远镜。在这款单筒望远镜之前，我从未使用过单筒望远镜，尽管我拥有它的时间还不长，但我已经非常满意了。虽然它非常适合观鸟和野外工作，但它也可以作为业余望远镜使用。<br /><br />这款单筒望远镜长约7英寸，足够大，可以使用，但又足够小，可以放进外套口袋甚至公文包。它配有一个漂亮的尼龙编织携带袋，甚至还有皮带环，因此可以轻松带入野外，不占用额外空间。它还有一个像摄像机一样的握带，可以更牢固地握住。它有一个小型金属三脚架，适合放在桌子或书桌上。这不是一个大三脚架——腿可延伸至7.5英寸——但如果你在录像或坐着需要稳定手部时，它效果很好。最后，盒子里还包括一块超细纤维清洁布，用于安全擦拭镜片而不会划伤。<br /><br />这是一款防水单筒望远镜，它的一切都体现了这一点。两端各有一个橡胶盖，覆盖镜片，在不使用时防止湿气和灰尘进入。这些盖子物理连接在镜筒上（一个通过橡胶翻盖，一个通过尼龙绳），所以不存在丢失盖子的问题。镜筒的大部分也是橡胶化的，除了调焦旋钮组件及其连接处。<br /><br />我用它聚焦房子后面的树线（大约30码远），对能够获得的清晰图像非常满意。从房子前面，我能够聚焦街道尽头的停车标志，即使在约一个足球场的距离上，也能获得精细的边缘细节。调焦非常平滑且易于调整，获得完美对焦一点也不困难。<br /><br />虽然我喜欢相机和光学设备，并且对光学设备有所了解，但我远非专家，所以我把这款单筒望远镜带到工作中，找了两位精通光学、摄影和天文学的队友，请他们检查并给出诚实的意见。他们两人都检查了它并表示认可。他们都说了类似的话：通过这款单筒望远镜看到的图像非常明亮清晰，调焦非常平滑，而且看起来制作精良。他们都评论了镜头，说它非常好，因为没有暗色涂层。<br /><br />我的一位队友让我尝试用它看星星。我并没有真正考虑过这个，但知道这将是一个极好的测试，所以天黑后我走到外面，将它指向天空。虽然通过专用望远镜我肯定能看到更多细节，但我能够辨认出月球上一些较大的表面细节，包括陨石坑中间的高点。

**解释**：产品：单筒望远镜｜声音：未提及声音相关内容｜其他：提及观鸟、观星、防水设计、携带袋、握带、三脚架、清洁布、调焦平滑、图像明亮清晰、镜头无暗色涂层、月球观测等。

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S3-096　G180

**原文**：Used them at first in the gym. Constantly interrupted sound, sometimes only in one, sometimes in both earpieces, with the phone ~1ft away. Thought maybe it's the congested environment with so much WiFi & Bluetooth traffic.<br />So yesterday I used them on the bicycle. Out in the open, nobody around, earphones ~3ft from my phone. Started out okay, but after 10mins same thing, music constantly cutting out. Complete junk. Glad I can return them.

**翻译**：最初在健身房使用它们。声音不断中断，有时只有一只耳机，有时两只都有，而手机距离约1英尺。以为可能是环境拥挤，WiFi和蓝牙流量太大。<br />所以昨天我在自行车上使用它们。在开阔地带，周围没人，耳机距离手机约3英尺。开始还好，但10分钟后同样的情况，音乐不断中断。完全垃圾。很高兴我能退货。

**解释**：产品：耳机｜声音：声音不断中断（constantly interrupted sound），音乐不断中断（music constantly cutting out），有时只有一只耳机，有时两只都有（sometimes only in one, sometimes in both earpieces）｜其他：在健身房和自行车上使用（used them at first in the gym, used them on the bicycle），手机距离（phone ~1ft away, earphones ~3ft from my phone），环境拥挤WiFi和蓝牙流量（congested environment with so much WiFi & Bluetooth traffic），开阔地带没人（out in the open, nobody around），开始还好（started out okay），10分钟后（after 10mins），完全垃圾（Complete junk），高兴能退货（Glad I can return them）

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-003　G182

**原文**：I couldn't be happier with this purchase. I bought this on sale for $139 and it's exactly what I needed. If you're looking for something reliable, fast, light, and thin when folded up, then this is just right. I was looking for something to bum around on the couch, check email, surf the web, use chrome cast, etc, and this performs wonderfully. Also bought a 64GB card from Amazon and have it installed as well. I really love using Windows 8.1 and it performs nicely on this device. Also came with 100GB Windows OneDrive for 2 years and Asus 500GB cloud storage. Seriously, you can't go wrong with this one. You also cannot beat the battery time on this, what is it, like 10 hours?!? The only negative I can say is I really like my wife's touch screen, which this doesn't have, but her's also cost double what mine did.

**翻译**：我对这次购买再满意不过了。我以139美元的价格在促销时买下了它，它正是我需要的。如果你在寻找可靠、快速、轻薄且折叠后很薄的东西，那么这款就正合适。我当时在找能在沙发上随便用用、查邮件、上网、用Chromecast等的东西，而这款表现极好。我还从亚马逊买了一张64GB的卡并装上了。我真的很喜欢用Windows 8.1，它在这台设备上运行得很好。还附带了2年的100GB Windows OneDrive和Asus 500GB云存储。说真的，选这款不会错。你也找不到比这更好的电池续航了，是多少来着，大概10小时？！我唯一能说的不足是我很喜欢我妻子的触摸屏，而这款没有，但她的价格也是我的两倍。

**解释**：产品：华硕Windows平板电脑｜声音：未提及｜其他：价格139美元促销购买；可靠、快速、轻薄、折叠后薄；用于沙发休闲、查邮件、上网、Chromecast；安装了64GB存储卡；Windows 8.1运行良好；附带2年100GB Windows OneDrive和Asus 500GB云存储；电池续航约10小时；没有触摸屏，而妻子的设备有触摸屏但价格是两倍

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S3-086　G194

**原文**：Poor design- speakers fire up & down in relation to ipod or only one speaker fires at you at 90* rotation. No power lite so easy to forget its on, low volume and tinny at max input. ## S1-062　G197

**原文**：I had written a nice review for this device...originally. Presently, even before one year has passed, the lock died. Because of this I must now NOT RECOMMEND this device, no matter how convenient it is for you. It's better that you carry your door key and gain entry that way. That or use Google to unlock your door from your car. I would say Alexa but the app does not play well with Android as an assistant app.  Until August comes out with a lock that works better in the winter AND had solar charging, I would not recommend this device for purchase.

**翻译**：我曾为这个设备写过一篇好评……原本是这样。现在，甚至不到一年，锁就坏了。因此，我现在必须不推荐这个设备，无论它对你来说有多方便。你最好还是随身带门钥匙，用那种方式进门。或者用谷歌从车里解锁门。我本想说是Alexa，但这款应用作为助手应用与安卓系统配合得不好。在August推出一款在冬天更好用并且有太阳能充电的锁之前，我不推荐购买这个设备。

**解释**：产品：不确定｜声音：（仅翻译，无解析：该条反复触发中性闸门，按兜底策略只提供翻译）｜其他：

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-046　G198

**原文**：I generally take care of my equipment and prefer for it to remain in good order for the entire time I own it, so I always pick up a tablet sleeve even if I rarely bring the device anywhere to begin with. This is a great, basic sleeve - plenty of space, plenty of padding, large zipper for easy zipping, and keeps the tablet out of the sun, dust, and moisture of an indoor environment. Material is a little coarse but doesn't feel cheap. Space for a charging cable and flattish USB charger in the front pocket. The sleeve easily fits my Galaxy Tab S4 while it is in its pseudo-leather case (ASIN: B07GF8K8ZW), which in turn is wide enough to have space for a stylus. .<br /><br />it is a soft sleeve and not rigid. This means that while you could keep a bare tablet in it, or a tablet with just a thin gel case on it, and it would protect it from the environment and random impacts, I would still recommend getting a proper case for the tablet itself if you are going to be traveling with it.

**翻译**：我通常会好好爱护我的设备，希望在我拥有它的整个期间都保持良好状态，所以即使我很少把设备带出门，我也总会买一个平板电脑保护套。这是一个很棒的基础款保护套——空间充足，衬垫厚实，大拉链便于拉合，能让平板电脑免受室内环境中的阳光、灰尘和湿气影响。材质有点粗糙，但不会让人觉得廉价。前袋有空间放充电线和扁平的USB充电器。这个保护套能轻松装下我套着仿皮保护壳（ASIN: B07GF8K8ZW）的Galaxy Tab S4，而那个保护壳本身又足够宽，可以放下一支触控笔。它是一个软质保护套，不是硬质的。这意味着虽然你可以把裸机平板电脑放进去，或者只套着一个薄硅胶壳的平板电脑放进去，它能保护设备免受环境和随机碰撞的影响，但如果你要带着平板电脑出行，我还是建议给平板电脑本身配一个合适的保护壳。

**解释**：产品：平板电脑保护套｜声音：未提及声音相关的内容｜其他：提到保护套的空间、衬垫、拉链、材质、防尘防潮、前袋可放充电线和USB充电器、可容纳套着仿皮保护壳的Galaxy Tab S4、软质非硬质、建议出行时给平板电脑配合适保护壳

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-030　G202

**原文**：Ordered to help with my own angst… My teenage son lives in basement at opposite end of the house. With door shut/music on and phone silenced- it’s impossible to reach without walking downstairs anytime I need to communicate.. I went looking for an easy fix to yelling or stomping on floor-<br /><br />GLCONN Walkie-talkies was the simple and ideal  solution! Best of all, when I need contact… It’s my voice he hears over the walkie without any need to answer or push any buttons- it’s leaves no escape from communication! Of course, to respond one simply needs to push in a button to do so. These have also proved helpful on larger size property we reside on in Utah- my husband can take this out to the north 40 letting me reach him with ease.. range is approx. 700yds. and we still have crystal clear com.’s- impressive!! These would be ideal for small businesses where a stock room or outdoor space is out of vocal range.. they are not commercial grade to suffice a large multi level building or campus- but these do the job far above a child’s toy for sure. Which brings up a useful idea if kids are outside playing on your street- These would be easy to keep contact both directions..Great to meet smaller need options.<br /><br />To use: simply turn on and program both walkies to the same channel. Easy to do- how do you change option a recorded voice lets you know exactly what channel you are on. Then it’s as easy as depressing the button to speak… Letting go to listen! Comes with rechargeable batteries and charge cord for both devices- fully charged gives users a green light. Note: these are still usable plugged in to a power source as well. We charge each night and battery lasts all day to avoid a low battery issue- keep things easy!<br /><br />Light weight- One won’t be encumbered with excess bulk it’s size of adult hand- save photos included for up close charging, size comp.’s, green light, etc.. each unit has belt clip and also can be connected to lanyard as well. Small enough to fit in pocket. Simple design has not much to it.. it’s what I like! Easy usability- 100% happy with purchase!!<br />Hoping this info may help in your decision- stay safe everyone.

**翻译**：为了缓解我自己的焦虑而订购的……我十几岁的儿子住在房子另一端的地下室。关着门、放着音乐、手机静音——每当我需要沟通时，不走到楼下就不可能联系到他。我一直在寻找一个简单的解决办法，不用大喊或跺地板——

GLCONN 对讲机就是简单而理想的解决方案！最棒的是，当我需要联系时……他听到的是我的声音通过对讲机传来，不需要接听或按任何按钮——这让沟通无处可逃！当然，要回应只需按下一个按钮即可。这些在我们居住的犹他州较大面积的房产上也很有用——我丈夫可以带着它去北边 40 英亩的地方，让我轻松联系到他……范围大约 700 码，我们仍然有清晰如水晶的通信——令人印象深刻！！这些对于小型企业来说也很理想，比如储藏室或户外空间超出了人声范围……它们不是商业级别，不足以覆盖大型多层建筑或园区——但这些肯定远胜于儿童玩具。这引出一个有用的想法：如果孩子们在你家街道外面玩耍——这些可以轻松保持双向联系……很高兴能满足较小需求的选择。

使用：只需打开并将两个对讲机编程到同一频道。很容易做到——如何更改选项，一个录音声音会告诉你确切在哪个频道。然后就像按下按钮说话一样简单……松开听！附带可充电电池和两个设备的充电线——充满电后用户会看到绿灯。注意：这些也可以插在电源上使用。我们每晚充电，电池能用一整天，避免低电量问题——保持简单！

轻便——不会因多余的体积而累赘，大小如成人手掌——保存了照片，包括近距离充电、尺寸比较、绿灯等。每个单元都有皮带夹，也可以连接挂绳。小到可以放进口袋。简单设计没有太多花哨……这正是我喜欢的！易于使用——100% 满意购买！！
希望这些信息能帮助您做决定——大家注意安全。

**解释**：产品：GLCONN 对讲机｜声音：评论者提到在约 700 码范围内仍有清晰如水晶的通信（crystal clear com.'s），并描述对讲机的声音功能，如录音声音会告知频道（a recorded voice lets you know exactly what channel you are on），以及按下按钮说话、松开听（depressing the button to speak… Letting go to listen）。｜其他：评论者还提到使用场景（联系地下室里的儿子、在犹他州房产上联系丈夫、小型企业、孩子户外玩耍）、使用方式（打开并编程到同一频道、按下按钮说话、松开听）、电池（可充电电池、充电线、充满电绿灯、每晚充电、电池用一整天、可插电使用）、物理特性（轻便、成人手掌大小、皮带夹、可连接挂绳、可放口袋、简单设计），以及购买满意度（100% 满意）。

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-083　G206

**原文**：Trying to decide if we were ready to cut the cord, we installed this antenna to see if we could get all the local channels.  We got 40 digital channels about 20 miles west of Washington DC and the major networks are crystal clear.<br /><br />The network towers we use are clustered together about 20 miles away.  We have 2 or more topographical obstructions between our house and the towers (no line of sight).  The Noise Margin on the networks is 35-40 dB.  TVFool.com listed my location as &#34;yellow&#34; (which means use an attic antenna or higher).  The cable drop is about 25 feet and I did not use an amplifier.<br /><br />This antenna is supposed to be omni-directional but we did experience occasional signal drop on the networks with the initial &#34;eyeball&#34; installation (which was off about 15 degrees).  Standing directly under the antenna and looking through a compass I had my son walk through the yard until he stepped into the path to the towers.  Then I had him stand still, facing the antenna, and rotated it until he told me it was pointed straight over his head.  That seemed to fix the signal drops.  Given all the factors of our situation, I am probably maxing out the performance of this antenna.  If I have any issues I'll just buy the add-on the reflector shield to increase directional performance...but for right now I don't need it.<br /><br />Installation was easy (I just mounted it to the Direct TV mast).  This antenna is small and light which was a big plus when climbing up a 25 foot ladder.<br /><br />So far so good!

**翻译**：为了决定我们是否准备好剪断有线电视线，我们安装了这个天线，看看能否收到所有本地频道。我们在华盛顿特区以西约20英里处收到了40个数字频道，主要网络非常清晰。<br /><br />我们使用的网络发射塔聚集在约20英里外。我们的房子和发射塔之间有两个或更多的地形障碍（没有视线）。网络的噪声容限为35-40 dB。TVFool.com将我的位置列为“黄色”（这意味着使用阁楼天线或更高）。电缆引线约25英尺，我没有使用放大器。<br /><br />这个天线应该是全向的，但在最初的“目测”安装中（偏离约15度），我们确实偶尔遇到网络信号中断。站在天线正下方，通过指南针，我让儿子在院子里走动，直到他踏入通往发射塔的路径。然后我让他站着不动，面向天线，并旋转它，直到他告诉我它正指向他的头顶。这似乎解决了信号中断问题。考虑到我们情况的所有因素，我可能正在最大化这个天线的性能。如果我有任何问题，我只需购买附加的反射器屏蔽来增加定向性能……但现在我不需要它。<br /><br />安装很容易（我只是把它安装到Direct TV桅杆上）。这个天线又小又轻，这在爬上25英尺梯子时是一个很大的优势。<br /><br />到目前为止一切顺利！

**解释**：产品：电视天线｜声音：主要网络非常清晰（crystal clear）；偶尔遇到网络信号中断（occasional signal drop on the networks）；这似乎解决了信号中断问题（That seemed to fix the signal drops）｜其他：收到40个数字频道；网络发射塔约20英里远；地形障碍；噪声容限35-40 dB；TVFool.com列为黄色；电缆引线25英尺；未使用放大器；全向天线；安装调整；安装容易；天线小轻；考虑购买反射器屏蔽

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S3-050　G208

**原文**：This isn't the advanced microscope that your chemistry class in college used.  It's better in some ways (easy link to smart phone, not as complicated to use for a beginner, etc) and not as good in others (at higher powers it isn't quite as crystal clear as my $400 name brand microscope is).  This is a good one for my kids, who just want to play.  It's also a good one for a student who wants to practice at home.  If you're a chemistry major who wants a top of the line model that will still meet their needs when they graduate and are working on research, this isn't the right model.  For the price, I think it was excellent!

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S3-057　G209

**原文**：I hate the price for what these are, maybe they were cheaper on a different day. But since the brand I usually buy does not make this specific item. Here I am and price aside. I am VERY happy with the throughput these offer. Don't see any power/data loss when using for my Steam Deck or the aftermarket dock. And since they make your already favorite cables magnetic USB-C. It should be the answer most need to try magnetic connector for their devices. And if your cables were already good for data/charge, these shouldn't change that unless you are using some very high spec stuff.<br />Ever since wearing out PS4 controller charge ports from the necessity of plugging charge cables that often get tugged from excited gaming, pets and just forgetting you are tethered. I now ONLY use higher end magnetic cables and dongles like these for charging my PS5/Switch/Steam Deck/Retroid or any other item that needs frequent charging where the connector could be damaged by movement. For home use and travel these are worth the purchase if you want to extend the life of USB-C charge ports.<br /><br />BTW, can't mention the brand here since this "Sheeast" brand only seems to sell the dongle and no cables (starts with K and sounds like a Hawaiian dance). But for magnetic cables for data/charging that are not total garbage. Only buy the elongated, almost rectangle magnetic cables. Like the shape of these dongles. AVOID the circle cables. And don't be careless and let the magnetic ends drag on the floor. They will collect metal and crud and become useless. Be good to your stuff!

**翻译**：我讨厌这些东西的价格，也许换个日子会更便宜。但因为我通常买的品牌不生产这个特定产品。所以我就来了，撇开价格不谈。我对它们提供的吞吐量非常满意。用于我的Steam Deck或售后底座时，没有看到任何电力/数据损失。而且由于它们能让你已经喜欢的电缆变成磁吸USB-C。这应该是大多数需要尝试磁吸连接器的人们的答案。如果你的电缆本来就适合数据/充电，这些不应该改变这一点，除非你使用的是非常高端规格的东西。<br />自从因为兴奋的游戏、宠物以及只是忘记你被拴住而经常拉扯充电电缆，导致PS4控制器充电端口磨损后。我现在只使用像这些这样的高端磁吸电缆和转接头来为我的PS5/Switch/Steam Deck/Retroid或任何其他需要频繁充电且连接器可能因移动而损坏的物品充电。对于家庭使用和旅行，如果你想延长USB-C充电端口的寿命，这些是值得购买的。<br /><br />顺便说一句，不能在这里提品牌，因为这个“Sheeast”品牌似乎只卖转接头而不卖电缆（以K开头，听起来像一种夏威夷舞蹈）。但对于不是完全垃圾的磁吸数据/充电电缆。只买细长的、几乎矩形的磁吸电缆。就像这些转接头的形状。避免圆形电缆。而且不要粗心大意让磁吸端拖在地上。它们会收集金属和污垢，变得无用。善待你的东西！

**解释**：产品：磁吸USB-C转接头｜声音：未提及｜其他：价格、吞吐量、电力/数据损失、Steam Deck、售后底座、PS4控制器充电端口磨损、PS5/Switch/Steam Deck/Retroid充电、延长USB-C充电端口寿命、品牌Sheeast、磁吸电缆形状建议、避免圆形电缆、维护磁吸端

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-033　G210

**原文**：All needed features while retaining a compact, but not too small, design.<br />I've had this unit 3 months and it performs extremely well. Maintains correct time, alarm works great, charges my Samsung 20 faster than my old Qi charger, dim function on clock display is well thought out, speaker clarity and volume are more than adequate, and the buttons/controls are easy to use.<br /><br />The buttons just make sense.  There's often a lot of functions within small electronics that we'll never use (often because we don't care or we throw out, lose, or don't want to bother consulting directions)...but you truly don't even need any directions on this unit to use all of its functions.<br /><br />Strongly recommend.

**翻译**：所有需要的功能，同时保持紧凑但不太小的设计。<br />我已经使用这个设备3个月了，它表现得非常好。保持正确的时间，闹钟工作得很好，给我的三星20充电比我的旧Qi充电器更快，时钟显示屏上的调光功能考虑得很周到，扬声器的清晰度和音量都足够，而且按钮/控件易于使用。<br /><br />按钮设计得很合理。小型电子产品中通常有很多我们永远不会使用的功能（通常是因为我们不在乎，或者我们扔掉、丢失，或者不想费心查阅说明）……但你真的不需要任何说明就能使用这个设备的所有功能。<br /><br />强烈推荐。

**解释**：产品：带闹钟和Qi充电功能的时钟收音机/扬声器｜声音：扬声器的清晰度和音量都足够（speaker clarity and volume are more than adequate）｜其他：保持了紧凑但不太小的设计；使用了3个月，表现非常好；保持正确时间；闹钟工作良好；给三星20充电比旧Qi充电器更快；时钟显示屏的调光功能考虑周到；按钮/控件易于使用；按钮设计合理；不需要说明书就能使用所有功能；强烈推荐。

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-052　G211

**原文**：I bought this just before holidays in 2015 and love the quality and the ease of use. I have used this LED TV to watch movies from my laptop and also watch satellite television on this smart LED TV. It has built in Wi-Fi and with internet connection to my router I am able to enjoy YouTube, Amazon video services, Netflix, Hulu Plus, Pandora, Crackle and many more. It has one HDMI and one USB Port. You can use a flash drive on the USB port to play music or video.<br /><br />Connecting this to the satellite receiver or the laptop is a breeze and the little manual that comes in the package is brief and very informative to get this baby started. You can also go online and get a more informative user’s manual which gives lot more details but you don’t really need that.<br /><br />This LED TV will work without internet but only as a regular TV; but you will have to get cable or satellite services. All smart TV's need Internet to use features like Netflix, Amazon, Crackle, WSJ, Yahoo Sports, Facebook, EBay, etc. I have used Crackle to watch many 1980s and 90s sitcoms. It is a joy to access these TV shows.<br /><br />The device is light and measures about 21.6&#34; x 13.8&#34; x 2.1 inches without stand. Very easy to setup and the package was delivered on time and the product was intact. Great buy and very reasonably priced. I am very satisfied with this purchase!

**翻译**：我在2015年假期前买了这个，喜欢它的质量和易用性。我用这台LED电视从笔记本电脑上看电影，也在这台智能LED电视上看卫星电视。它有内置Wi-Fi，通过连接到我的路由器，我可以享受YouTube、Amazon视频服务、Netflix、Hulu Plus、Pandora、Crackle等等。它有一个HDMI和一个USB端口。你可以使用USB端口上的闪存驱动器来播放音乐或视频。<br /><br />将它连接到卫星接收器或笔记本电脑轻而易举，包装中附带的小手册简短且非常有信息量，可以让这个宝贝启动起来。你也可以上网获取更详细的用户手册，其中提供了更多细节，但你真的不需要那个。<br /><br />这台LED电视可以在没有互联网的情况下工作，但只能作为普通电视；但你必须获得有线或卫星服务。所有智能电视都需要互联网才能使用Netflix、Amazon、Crackle、WSJ、Yahoo Sports、Facebook、EBay等功能。我用Crackle看了许多1980年代和90年代的情景喜剧。访问这些电视节目是一种乐趣。<br /><br />该设备很轻，尺寸约为21.6英寸 x 13.8英寸 x 2.1英寸（不含支架）。非常容易设置，包装按时送达，产品完好无损。很棒的购买，价格非常合理。我对这次购买非常满意！

**解释**：产品：LED电视｜声音：未提及｜其他：质量、易用性、内置Wi-Fi、流媒体服务（YouTube、Amazon、Netflix、Hulu Plus、Pandora、Crackle）、HDMI和USB端口、连接卫星接收器和笔记本电脑、手册、尺寸、设置、交付、价格、满意度

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-005　G212

**原文**：I bought this SDR device so I could listen to shortwave radio but I still can't get that band to work. I have tried the HDSDR software and some other software but it only seems to go as low as 25MHZ. I tried switching where the antenna is connected and it doesn't make a difference. It works a lot better than my other SDR device and has better reception but neither of my SDR devices will pick up shortwave. Is there a software or setting that you recommend for this??<br /><br />One other issue I noticed is the board inside the case seems loose. It rattles around. This isn't a big problem, just something I want to note. It has had a software crash a couple of times and I'm wondering if the loose board is causing this...<br /><br />So far I am really liking this SDR device though...

**翻译**：我买了这个SDR设备，以便能收听短波电台，但我仍然无法让那个波段工作。我试过HDSDR软件和其他一些软件，但它似乎只能低至25MHz。我尝试更换天线连接的位置，但没有任何区别。它比我的另一个SDR设备工作得好得多，接收效果也更好，但我的两个SDR设备都无法接收短波。您有推荐的软件或设置吗？？<br /><br />我注意到的另一个问题是，外壳内的电路板似乎松动了。它摇晃时会发出响声。这不是大问题，只是我想提一下。它已经崩溃过几次软件，我想知道是不是松动的电路板导致了这个问题...<br /><br />不过到目前为止，我真的很喜欢这个SDR设备...

**解释**：产品：SDR设备｜声音：接收效果更好（better reception），但无法接收短波（can't get that band to work），只能低至25MHz（only seems to go as low as 25MHZ）｜其他：软件问题（HDSDR软件和其他软件），天线连接位置（switching where the antenna is connected），电路板松动（board inside the case seems loose），软件崩溃（software crash）

## S2-010　G216

**原文**：They were a little tricky to pair with an ipad.  I don't see the app as particularly helpful for these.<br /><br />The best feature is one of the swap on ear pads.  It looks a little more matte than the others with a visible tiny circle in it.  Those crush up like earplugs, and then exactly like earplugs expand to keep the earbud in place.  Those are also the most  noise reducing.  I can't say noise cancelling.  These aren't that.  But it does help keep of the ambient noise down.  My main concern was one falling out, but with that pad, that hasn't been a problem and I doubt it will be.<br /><br />I had no problem with ear buds disconnecting from each other.  Once they got started, they were rock solid.  I got about two hours worth of use out of one charge, and then ran out of time to listen.  I think for me they will last long enough, and the case holds a charge for powering up on the go.  It uses a standard USB cable.  I like things that follow an industry standard.

**翻译**：它们与iPad配对时有点棘手。我不觉得这个应用对它们特别有帮助。<br /><br />最好的功能之一是其中一种可更换的耳塞垫。它看起来比其他耳塞垫更哑光，上面有一个可见的小圆圈。那些耳塞垫像耳塞一样压扁，然后完全像耳塞一样膨胀，以保持耳塞在适当位置。那些也是最能降低噪音的。我不能说是降噪。这些不是那样。但它确实有助于降低环境噪音。我主要担心的是耳塞会掉出来，但有了那个耳塞垫，这不成问题，我怀疑也不会成为问题。<br /><br />我没有遇到耳塞彼此断开连接的问题。一旦它们开始工作，它们非常稳定。一次充电我用了大约两个小时，然后就没有时间听了。我认为对我来说它们会持续足够长的时间，而且充电盒可以保持电量以便随时充电。它使用标准USB线。我喜欢遵循行业标准的东西。

**解释**：产品：无线耳塞｜声音：最能降低噪音的（most noise reducing），不能说是降噪（can't say noise cancelling），有助于降低环境噪音（help keep of the ambient noise down）｜其他：与iPad配对有点棘手（a little tricky to pair with an ipad），应用不太有帮助（app not particularly helpful），可更换耳塞垫（swap on ear pads），耳塞垫像耳塞一样膨胀（expand like earplugs），防止耳塞掉落（falling out），耳塞连接稳定（rock solid），一次充电约两小时（about two hours worth of use out of one charge），充电盒保持电量（case holds a charge），使用标准USB线（standard USB cable）

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-067　G217

**原文**：I was looking for a simple sort of cd clock radio to keep on a nightstand.  It's amazing how difficult it was to find something like that that fit my needs, and I ended up with this unit although I really wasn't intrested in the iPhone/iPod connections.  I went to a local electronics store and first tried a litte Sony machine, was set to buy it, and then the salesman showed me this Phillips unit.  The sound was overwhelmingly better on the Phillips unit.  I admit I'm not an audiophile, but I just want something that sounds good when I played CDs at bedtime.  I quickly dismissed the little Sony and went for this unit.  Since having it about a week now, I really like the sound from the CD player.  I did try my iPhone 4 on it to see if it would connect, and it did and it played music like a champ.  Strong and clear.  I have a rubber bumper on my iPhone and had to remove it to make the phone fit properly but that's no big deal.  In fact what I'm thinking of doing is reprogramming my older iPhone (first generation) with nothing but music on it and leaving that plugged in the Phillips unit.  I keep my iPhone 4 in a different charger in a different room so it doesn't disrupt my sleep AND so I won't forget it when I'm leaving the house.  So far I'm a happy camper with this little Philips unit.  The only con is the brightness of the backlit display which is rather bright.  I placed the unit in a different part of the room where the remote still works but the brightness of the display doesn't affect my sleep.  I like a dark room!

**翻译**：我当时在找一种简单的CD时钟收音机，放在床头柜上。要找到符合我需求的东西竟然这么难，最后我买了这台，尽管我其实对iPhone/iPod连接功能并不感兴趣。我去了一家本地电子产品商店，先试了一台小索尼机器，本来打算买它，然后销售员给我看了这台飞利浦。飞利浦的音质压倒性地更好。我承认我不是发烧友，但我只是想要在睡前播放CD时听起来不错的东西。我很快放弃了那台小索尼，选择了这台。用了大约一周后，我非常喜欢CD播放器的声音。我确实试了我的iPhone 4连接它，看看能不能连上，结果连上了，播放音乐非常棒。强劲而清晰。我的iPhone上有个橡胶保护套，不得不取下来才能让手机合适地放进去，但这没什么大不了的。事实上，我在考虑把我旧iPhone（第一代）重新编程，只放音乐，然后一直插在飞利浦上。我把iPhone 4放在另一个房间的另一个充电器上，这样它不会干扰我的睡眠，而且我出门时也不会忘记带它。到目前为止，我对这台小飞利浦很满意。唯一的缺点是背光显示屏的亮度相当亮。我把机器放在房间的另一个位置，遥控器仍然有效，但显示屏的亮度不影响我的睡眠。我喜欢黑暗的房间！

**解释**：产品：CD时钟收音机｜声音：评论者说飞利浦的音质比索尼好得多（“The sound was overwhelmingly better on the Phillips unit”），喜欢CD播放器的声音（“I really like the sound from the CD player”），iPhone播放音乐强劲而清晰（“Strong and clear”）。｜其他：评论者提到寻找简单CD时钟收音机的困难，对iPhone/iPod连接功能不感兴趣，在商店比较了索尼和飞利浦，使用iPhone 4连接并播放音乐，需要取下橡胶保护套才能放入，考虑将旧iPhone重新编程只放音乐并一直插着，将iPhone 4放在另一个房间充电以避免干扰睡眠和忘记带，唯一缺点是背光显示屏太亮，将机器移到房间另一部分以避免影响睡眠，喜欢黑暗的房间。

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-062　G218

**原文**：These are very handy to have to minimize (or eliminate) cord tangles.  The coil is tight and holds its shape well.  When &#34;at rest&#34; (e,g., not stretched) the cable is about 26 inches end to end. You can get it to 12 feet if you stretch it out fully, but the coil strength is such that it will pull whatever it is attached to.  If you need the full 12 foot length, it would be better to get a non-coiled cable.<br /><br />The ends are well attached, and the wire sheath seems pretty durable.  Zero complaints about quality -- no crackling even when I jostle the ends while they are plugged in.<br /><br />A great way to add some positioning flexibility for attached speakers without having to deal with excess cable that gets tangled. Highly recommend.<br />[Sample provided for review]

**翻译**：这些非常方便，可以最小化（或消除）线缆缠绕。线圈紧密且能很好地保持形状。在“静止”状态下（例如，未拉伸时），线缆两端之间大约26英寸。如果完全拉伸，可以达到12英尺，但线圈的强度会拉动它所连接的任何东西。如果你需要完整的12英尺长度，最好选择非卷曲的线缆。<br /><br />端头连接牢固，线材护套似乎相当耐用。对质量零抱怨——即使我在插着的时候晃动端头，也没有杂音。<br /><br />这是一种很好的方式，可以为连接的扬声器增加一些定位灵活性，而不必处理会缠绕的多余线缆。强烈推荐。<br />[样品提供用于评测]

## S2-061　G219

**原文**：Good value for a keyboard and mouse. The mouse is comfortable to use and quiet. The last mouse I was using had developed a squeak when using the scroll wheel - very annoying. My main complaint about this set is that the keyboard could be much quieter. I work from home and use the keyboard all day long and it does get a little loud sometimes. However, a lot of the quieter keyboards I looked at had a funny keyboard layout. The layout on this keyboard is great and very comfortable to use.

## S2-079　G220

**原文**：Great projector at a great price. I thought I would be annoyed at the light surrounding the image that others have mentioned, but with a black wall and black curtains, I don't even notice it. The only thing that really bothers me is the rainbow effect (I'm one of those people). Other than that, the image is vivid, bright with great contrast and the black level is quite good. It's also very quiet and the fan only runs for about 20 seconds after shutdown. I use this mounted on the ceiling with an HDMI wireless receiver with no trouble at all.

**翻译**：很棒的投影仪，价格也很棒。我原以为我会对其他人提到的图像周围的光线感到烦恼，但在黑墙和黑色窗帘的衬托下，我甚至注意不到它。唯一真正困扰我的是彩虹效应（我就是那种人）。除此之外，图像生动、明亮，对比度极佳，黑位也相当不错。它也非常安静，风扇在关机后只运行大约20秒。我把它吊装在天花板上，配合HDMI无线接收器使用，完全没有任何问题。

**解释**：产品：投影仪｜声音：非常安静（very quiet），风扇在关机后只运行大约20秒（the fan only runs for about 20 seconds after shutdown）｜其他：价格（great price）、图像周围的光线（light surrounding the image）、黑墙和黑色窗帘（black wall and black curtains）、彩虹效应（rainbow effect）、图像生动明亮（image is vivid, bright）、对比度（great contrast）、黑位（black level）、吊装在天花板上（mounted on the ceiling）、HDMI无线接收器（HDMI wireless receiver）

## S1-024　G221

## S2-045　G226

**原文**：My family got together and picked this up for me during Fuji's sale/promotion in March. This is going to cover Christmas and birthdays for the next few years  :)<br /><br />Build quality is top notch. The zoom, focus and aperture rings are all nicely sized and very... grippy. The zoom itself is almost, but not quite, too stiff. I noticed no creep whatsoever. Autofocus is, expectedly, a bit slower than the kit lens but still decent.  Image stabilization is impressive enough for hand-held shooting in low light at full zoom. Image quality is impressive. The included hood is huge, seriously huge. It's actually a little hard to get the lens cap on with the hood on. Speaking of the lens hood; my one gripe with this rig is when fully 'on' the lens the hood tends to rattle a little bit. I've found that backing off the connection a 1/4 inch or so keeps the hood from rattling and still keeps it firmly in place.<br /><br />It does make my X-E2 a little front heavy but it's not that horrible. I'm contemplating buying one of the extension grips from Fuji for the camera. A nicely padded neck strap is a definite plus when carrying a camera with this lens mounted on it. One thing I've had to pay attention to is when switching back to my 18-55mm lens I need to be careful of how hard I twist the barrel to zoom in and out this lens is MUCH stiffer to use than the kit 18-55mm that came with my X-E2. I worry I may break the smaller lens after using this lens for an extended period.<br /><br />Highly recommended.

**翻译**：我的家人在三月份富士的促销活动中聚在一起为我买了这个。这将覆盖未来几年的圣诞节和生日礼物：）<br /><br />做工质量是一流的。变焦、对焦和光圈环的尺寸都很好，而且非常……有抓握感。变焦本身几乎，但还不至于太紧。我完全没有注意到任何滑动。自动对焦，如预期的那样，比套机镜头稍慢，但仍然不错。图像稳定功能足够令人印象深刻，可以在低光下全变焦手持拍摄。图像质量令人印象深刻。附带的遮光罩很大，真的很大。实际上，装上遮光罩后，镜头盖有点难盖上。说到遮光罩；我对这个装备的唯一不满是，当完全‘安装’在镜头上时，遮光罩往往会有点晃动。我发现将连接处后退大约1/4英寸可以防止遮光罩晃动，并且仍然保持牢固。<br /><br />它确实让我的X-E2有点前重，但并不是那么糟糕。我正在考虑从富士购买一个相机扩展手柄。当携带装有这个镜头的相机时，一个衬垫良好的颈带绝对是一个加分项。我不得不注意的一件事是，当切换回我的18-55mm镜头时，我需要小心旋转镜筒来变焦的力度，这个镜头使用起来比我的X-E2附带的18-55mm套机镜头要硬得多。我担心在使用这个镜头一段时间后，我可能会弄坏较小的镜头。<br /><br />强烈推荐。

**解释**：产品：富士镜头｜声音：未提及声音｜其他：提及做工质量、变焦/对焦/光圈环、自动对焦速度、图像稳定、图像质量、遮光罩大小和晃动、相机前重、扩展手柄、颈带、与18-55mm镜头的比较。

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-073　G229

**原文**：I think I'll just do a simple pro and con list for this one.<br /><br />Pros:<br /><br /> + It starts up fast when switched on and immediately reconnects to the last Bluetooth device selected.<br /><br />+ The ability to store multiple device profiles and switch between them with the press of a button is awesome, and it saves a lot of time over having to open the Bluetooth settings on each device to reconnect.<br /><br /> + And of course it has a 2.4g wireless USB dongle, so you can use it with things that don't have Bluetooth or which require a stronger wireless signal.<br /><br /> + There are visible-yet-unobtrusive lights on the top that allow you to tell at a glance which device pairing you have selected.<br /><br /> + The shape of the mouse fits my hand perfectly and is very comfortable.<br /><br /> + The scroll wheel is easy to grip and roll back and forth. It has a very satisfying sort of "bump" feeling as it rotates from one position to another, whereas some wheels just turn freely, leaving you little control over how much you're actually scrolling. And most importantly, the wheel doesn't rock side-to-side like those ones that supposedly allow you to scroll left and right but never seem to work except by accident when you don't want them to.<br /><br /> + There's no branding that I can identify on the packaging, the instructions, nor on the mouse itself. I see this as a pro because well, frankly, a lot of these random brand names are weird and bother me (though admittedly, I did once buy a product on Amazon primarily because the brand name was BULLETFACE *cue electric guitar solo*).<br /><br /> + I really appreciate how every feature on this mouse serves a purpose. There are no dumb shapes or graphics added just to make it look "cool". It's cool enough as it is.<br /><br /> + It's great that it has a built-in rechargeable battery and a USB-C port on the front so that I can charge it wherever I can charge my phone. But because there's no battery access door, the dongle rests in a recess on the underside of the mouse and is held in by a really strong magnet. That's better than other attachment methods I've seen where the dongle either snaps in with plastic clips or press-fits into a slot, as those will both wear out and fail eventually, where a magnet will not.<br /><br />Cons:<br /><br /> - The ball feels a bit loose and rattle-y in its socket. It's not an issue when the mouse is on a level surface, but if you're carrying it around or trying to use it on the arm of a sofa, it can wiggle a bit, which feels weird.<br /><br /> - All but the lowest DPI setting is way too fast for anything I can imagine doing, but maybe I'm just not used to it yet. (Update: I guess I wasn't used to it. I've since moved up to the middle DPI setting now that I've gotten better at making fine movements)<br /><br /> - This may not be a con for some people, but the dongle really, really stays put in a USB port, to the point I was afraid I'd need pliers to get it out (though I managed to do so without them).<br /><br /> - Update: I thought of one more con. It's a shame I can't re-bind the little forward and back buttons on the far left to something more useful, like shift or ctrl-z.<br /><br />Conclusion:<br /><br />I'm currently very satisfied with my purchase, and I expect to get a lot of use out of this mouse for years to come. The best thing I can do to thank the manufacturer and seller for making a great product at a great price is to leave my honest feedback to help others find the product, so that's exactly what I've done. Oh, and I swyped (typed via path input) this entire review on my phone's on-screen keyboard using the very trackball I'm reviewing, if that gives you an idea of how comfortable and accurate it is to operate.

**翻译**：我想我就为这个产品做一个简单的优缺点列表吧。<br /><br />优点：<br /><br /> + 打开时启动很快，并且立即重新连接到上次选择的蓝牙设备。<br /><br />+ 能够存储多个设备配置文件并通过按一个按钮在它们之间切换，这太棒了，比必须在每个设备上打开蓝牙设置来重新连接节省了很多时间。<br /><br /> + 当然，它有一个2.4g无线USB接收器，所以你可以用它连接没有蓝牙或需要更强无线信号的设备。<br /><br /> + 顶部有可见但不显眼的指示灯，让你一眼就能看出你选择了哪个设备配对。<br /><br /> + 鼠标的形状完美贴合我的手，非常舒适。<br /><br /> + 滚轮易于抓握和前后滚动。它从一个位置旋转到另一个位置时有一种非常令人满意的“顿挫”感，而有些滚轮只是自由转动，让你几乎无法控制实际滚动了多少。最重要的是，滚轮不会像那些 supposedly 允许左右滚动但似乎除了意外时从不工作的滚轮那样左右摇晃。<br /><br /> + 我在包装、说明书或鼠标本身上都找不到任何品牌标识。我认为这是一个优点，因为坦率地说，很多这些随机的品牌名称很奇怪，让我烦恼（尽管我承认，我曾经在亚马逊上买过一个产品，主要因为品牌名称是BULLETFACE *电吉他独奏*）。<br /><br /> + 我真的很欣赏这个鼠标的每个功能都有其用途。没有为了让它看起来“酷”而添加的愚蠢形状或图形。它本身就已经够酷了。<br /><br /> + 它有一个内置可充电电池和前面的USB-C端口，这样我可以在任何能给手机充电的地方给它充电，这很好。但由于没有电池仓门，接收器放在鼠标底部的一个凹槽里，由一块非常强的磁铁固定。这比我见过的其他固定方法更好，那些方法要么用塑料夹子卡住接收器，要么压入槽中，这些最终都会磨损和失效，而磁铁不会。<br /><br />缺点：<br /><br /> - 球在它的窝里感觉有点松，嘎嘎响。当鼠标在水平表面上时这不是问题，但如果你带着它走动或试图在沙发扶手上使用它，它会有点晃动，感觉很奇怪。<br /><br /> - 除了最低的DPI设置外，其他设置对于我能想象的任何操作来说都太快了，但也许我只是还没习惯。（更新：我想我是不习惯。既然我已经更擅长做精细动作，我现在已经调到中间的DPI设置了）<br /><br /> - 对某些人来说这可能不是缺点，但接收器真的非常牢固地插在USB端口里，以至于我担心需要钳子才能把它拔出来

**解释**：产品：无线鼠标｜声音：未提及｜其他：启动速度、蓝牙重连、多设备配置文件切换、2.4g无线USB接收器、指示灯、鼠标形状舒适度、滚轮手感、无品牌标识、功能实用性、内置可充电电池、USB-C端口、接收器磁吸收纳、球体松动、DPI设置、接收器插拔紧

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-014　G232

**原文**：What I like about this watch:<br />- one full charge lasts several days<br />- good way to track your BPM, Blood pressure, calories burn, steps taken<br />- could make phone calls with the watch<br />- if you take the watch off for whatever reason (like to shower) it doesn't keep taking your BMP or blood pressure so you don't have to worry about your data getting messed up/ inaccurate.<br />- comfortable silicone band which has a lot of holes to adjust to your size wrist, I have a very small wrist and use the second to last hole<br />- good size screen, not too large and not too small<br />- you could set alarms (through the app) also features a stop watch and timer<br />- receive and check notifications for several apps plus text messages<br />- has a find phone feature if you misplace your phone<br />- you could add music to your phone (which I have not done, and not sure if I'm going to do)<br />- waterproof<br />- Compatible with my Samsung smartphone, and easy to set up<br />- Great to have at work, to keep track of time and if I receive any messages/notifications on my phone without having to check my phone<br /><br />dislike:<br />- only 4 different dial face designs to choose from<br />- speaker is towards the bottom so sound could be blocked by your arm depending on your position<br />- sleep tracker is not very accurate (while I'm laying in bed reading, watching a movie, or on my phone it thinks I am sleeping, and there is no way to edit the hours manually)<br />- no power button (to turn on or off)<br />- you cannot name or write notes with the alarms, there are only icons to label<br />- does not stay connected to my phone in my whole house. If I have my phone on charge in my living room and I'm in my kitchen which is 2 rooms over, I will not get notifications from my phone<br />- app doesn't not always update, I will have to go to the app and refresh (down swipe) for data to refresh and update<br />- charger connection is magnetic and a couple times it was bumped while on charge and the charger got knocked off and I didn't know, do it wasnt charging. Plus if the charger breaks for whatever reason I don't have a back up.

**翻译**：我喜欢这款手表的地方：<br />- 一次充满电可以用好几天<br />- 追踪心率、血压、卡路里消耗、步数的好方法<br />- 可以用手表打电话<br />- 如果因为某些原因摘下手表（比如洗澡），它不会继续测量心率或血压，所以不用担心数据被搞乱或不准确。<br />- 舒适的硅胶表带，有很多孔可以调节到适合你的手腕尺寸，我的手腕非常小，用倒数第二个孔<br />- 屏幕大小合适，不大也不小<br />- 可以设置闹钟（通过应用程序），还有秒表和计时器功能<br />- 接收和查看多个应用程序的通知以及短信<br />- 有查找手机功能，如果你把手机放错地方了<br />- 可以往手机里添加音乐（我还没试过，也不确定会不会试）<br />- 防水<br />- 与我的三星智能手机兼容，设置简单<br />- 工作时很好用，可以看时间，也能在不看手机的情况下收到手机上的消息/通知<br /><br />不喜欢的地方：<br />- 只有4种不同的表盘设计可选<br />- 扬声器在底部，所以根据你的姿势，声音可能会被手臂挡住<br />- 睡眠追踪不太准确（当我躺在床上看书、看电影或玩手机时，它以为我在睡觉，而且没有办法手动编辑时间）<br />- 没有电源按钮（用来开机或关机）<br />- 不能给闹钟命名或写备注，只有图标可以标记<br />- 在我整个房子里不能一直保持与手机连接。如果我把手机放在客厅充电，而我在厨房，隔了两个房间，我就收不到手机的通知<br />- 应用程序不总是更新，我得进入应用程序并刷新（下拉）才能刷新和更新数据<br />- 充电器连接是磁性的，有几次充电时被碰到，充电器掉了，我不知道，所以没在充电。而且如果充电器因为任何原因坏了，我没有备用。

**解释**：产品：智能手表｜声音：扬声器位于底部，因此根据手臂位置，声音可能会被遮挡（speaker is towards the bottom so sound could be blocked by your arm depending on your position）。｜其他：电池续航、健康追踪（心率、血压、卡路里、步数）、通话功能、佩戴检测、表带舒适度与调节、屏幕尺寸、闹钟/秒表/计时器、通知、查找手机、音乐存储、防水、兼容性与设置、工作使用、表盘设计、睡眠追踪准确性、电源按钮、闹钟标签、连接范围、应用刷新、充电器连接与备用。

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S3-006　G234

**原文**：I wouldn't buy this kit or recommend it to anyone. Even with all three lights on, it's not bright enough at all for shooting in manual. You can maybe get some good close up headshots by moving the lights so close to their face they are sweating, but that's about it. The backdrop support is nice, however. And if you can get your hands on a steamer, it will get the wrinkles out of the muslin. You can also get some piping from lowes to roll them up for storage so they wont wrinkle or get dirty. I might try to see if I can find brighter bulbs, but so far this was a waste of money.

**翻译**：我不会购买这个套装，也不会推荐给任何人。即使三盏灯全开，对于手动拍摄来说也完全不够亮。也许你可以把灯移得离脸非常近，近到他们出汗，才能拍出一些好的特写头像，但也就这样了。背景支架不错，然而。如果你能弄到一台蒸汽机，它可以去除薄纱上的皱纹。你还可以从劳氏买一些管子，把它们卷起来存放，这样就不会起皱或弄脏。我可能会尝试看看能不能找到更亮的灯泡，但到目前为止，这是浪费钱。

**解释**：产品：摄影灯套装｜声音：未提及声音｜其他：亮度不足，背景支架不错，去除薄纱皱纹，存放建议，寻找更亮灯泡，浪费钱

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-027　G240

**原文**：I bought my example of this fisheye lens from ebay for the EOS mount. I use this on a 1.6x crop and I have also used it on a full frame camera as well. It's not good below f11, but it is good at f16. Ken Rockwell mentions this in his review of this lens. With this lens its best to increase ISO even at the expense of image noise rather than open up the aperture beyond f8. I have tried to use this lens at f2.8 in low light on a 1.6x crop camera and it just does not look good. Not only is there a large loss of sharpness but for some reason the image becomes more orange on my example. I should mention that with my ebay example the coating on the front element wasn't even, so it's possible that it might be from that.<br /><br />The lens cap on mine doesn't stay on do to a broken tab, which can be annoying since it's specific to the lens.<br /><br />I think this lens is a good deal, if it can be had for cheap. I'm not sure I would buy another one if I lost mine at the current prices that this lens goes for though. The primary reason would be because a fisheye lens has limited use do to barrel distortion, I'd rather put that money towards a rectilinear wide angle.

**翻译**：我从eBay上购买了这个鱼眼镜头的EOS卡口版本。我在1.6倍裁切系数的相机上使用它，也在全画幅相机上使用过。它在f11以下表现不好，但在f16时表现良好。Ken Rockwell在他对这个镜头的评测中提到了这一点。使用这个镜头时，最好提高ISO，即使以图像噪点为代价，也不要将光圈开到f8以上。我曾尝试在低光环境下在1.6倍裁切相机上以f2.8使用这个镜头，但看起来就是不好。不仅锐度大幅下降，而且不知为何，在我的这个镜头上图像变得更橙。我应该提到，我eBay上买的这个镜头，前镜片的镀膜不均匀，所以可能与此有关。<br /><br />我的镜头盖因为一个卡扣断了而盖不紧，这可能会很烦人，因为它是这个镜头专用的。<br /><br />我认为这个镜头很划算，如果能便宜买到的话。但如果我丢失了我的镜头，以目前这个镜头的价格，我不确定是否会再买一个。主要原因是鱼眼镜头由于桶形畸变而用途有限，我宁愿把钱花在直线广角镜头上。

**解释**：产品：鱼眼镜头｜声音：未提及声音｜其他：提及在1.6倍裁切和全画幅相机上使用；在f11以下表现不好，f16时好；建议提高ISO而非开大光圈超过f8；在f2.8低光下锐度下降且图像变橙；前镜片镀膜不均匀；镜头盖卡扣断裂；认为划算但用途有限，更倾向于直线广角镜头。

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S3-028　G241

**原文**：For the most part this device is perfect.  I love the clean white, "Apple product" design.  It looks great and it has worked great as a short extension cord/charging center when testing at home.  It has a nice, stacking set of adapters that stores well in the thick storage/organization bag.  The bag is pretty nice but a little bulky like the charger itself.  I would have liked to see it a little slimmer and lighter for better packability for when every ounce and cubic inch is at a premium, but it seems pretty well built (the plug adapters are a little cheaper looking) and well designed, just a little big.

**翻译**：大部分情况下这个设备很完美。我喜欢干净的白色、“苹果产品”式的设计。它看起来很棒，在家里测试时作为短延长线/充电中心也工作得很好。它有一套不错的、可堆叠的适配器，可以很好地存放在厚实的存储/整理袋中。这个袋子相当不错，但和充电器本身一样有点笨重。我希望它能更薄更轻一些，以便在每盎司和每立方英寸都很宝贵时更好地打包，但它似乎做工相当好（插头适配器看起来有点廉价）且设计精良，只是有点大。

**解释**：产品：充电器/充电中心｜声音：未提及｜其他：设计（干净的白色、“苹果产品”式设计）、外观（看起来很棒）、功能（作为短延长线/充电中心工作良好）、适配器（可堆叠、存放良好）、存储袋（不错但有点笨重）、尺寸和重量（希望更薄更轻、有点大）、做工（似乎做工相当好，插头适配器看起来有点廉价）、设计（设计精良）

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S1-039　G244

**原文**：My son said these had terrible noise cancellation and he can not use them. They are basically like walking around with speakers on your head! He tried to use them at the library and people 30 feet away could hear what he was listening to! He often uses speakers to make calls on his phone too and that was not even possible.<br />They defeat the purpose of being headphones if they are simply speakers for all to hear.<br />He had to use cheap earbuds the library loaned him.<br />He said these grey and tan ones looked a bit girlish. I as a mom thought they were attractive. I think the tan may look a bit like light pink possibly and he said girls gave him compliments lol which may mean they are more to a girls liking. I got this color due to he is on the street a lot and can not be drawing attention to himself. He can not have red or blue gang colors which some of the others had even if it was in the cord. And the black ones had shiny chrome which is too much of an attention getter. So, I opted for the grey ones.<br />They are also bulky and if you are limited on space my son said they did not fit in his backpack well. One night he even gave them to me to take home since he needed room for more important items in his backpack. I told him to just put them on his neck. And he was not even interested.<br />So, I'd say they are a fail.

**翻译**：我儿子说这些耳机的降噪效果很糟糕，他没法用。基本上就像头上顶着扬声器到处走！他试着在图书馆用，结果30英尺外的人都能听到他在听什么！他经常用扬声器在手机上打电话，这甚至都不可能。如果它们只是让所有人都能听到的扬声器，那就失去了作为耳机的意义。他不得不使用图书馆借给他的廉价耳塞。他说这些灰色和棕色的看起来有点女孩子气。作为妈妈，我觉得它们挺好看的。我觉得棕色可能看起来有点像浅粉色，他说女孩们夸过他，哈哈，这可能意味着它们更受女孩喜欢。我选这个颜色是因为他经常在街上，不能引起注意。他不能有红色或蓝色帮派颜色，其他一些耳机即使是在线缆上也有这些颜色。而黑色的有闪亮的铬，太引人注目了。所以，我选了灰色的。它们也很笨重，如果你空间有限，我儿子说它们不能很好地放进他的背包。有一天晚上他甚至把它们给我带回家，因为他需要空间放更重要的东西。我告诉他挂在脖子上就行。他甚至不感兴趣。所以，我会说它们是个失败。

**解释**：产品：耳机｜声音：降噪效果很糟糕（terrible noise cancellation），像头上顶着扬声器（like walking around with speakers on your head），30英尺外的人都能听到（people 30 feet away could hear），无法用于打电话（not even possible），只是让所有人都能听到的扬声器（speakers for all to hear）｜其他：颜色外观（灰色和棕色，看起来有点女孩子气，女孩们夸过，棕色可能像浅粉色），帮派颜色限制（不能有红色或蓝色），黑色有闪亮铬太引人注目，笨重且背包空间有限，儿子不感兴趣挂在脖子上，整体是个失败

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

**原文**：I was in need of a durable long charging cord to use with Android Auto in my new car. My old cord kept randomly disconnecting from the system even though the cable was relatively new and in good condition visibility. ## S1-093　G254

**原文**：This isn't a bag that will walk the runway but it will take you to work, school and the library. It is a large bucket bag that is designed for service not style. Its lines are somewhat inelegant but it provides plenty of room and compartments for your electronics, accessories, files, notebooks and pads. The zipper is smooth and operates without catching. The shoulder straps are adjustable. and quite comfortable. The exterior is described as slate and is a charcoal gray color with a lighter gray interior. It is a practical color that is neutral and hides dirts and mars with ease. The metal feet on the bottom keep it standing upright. the biggest disappointment is that it is made of pleather, not leather as advertised. But if you value function over fashion and can get it at a good price, it is a solid, well made working bag.

**翻译**：这不是一个会走上T台的包，但它会带你去上班、上学和图书馆。它是一个大号水桶包，设计用于服务而非风格。它的线条有些不够优雅，但为你的电子产品、配件、文件、笔记本和便签本提供了充足的空间和隔层。拉链顺滑，操作时不卡顿。肩带可调节，而且相当舒适。外部被描述为石板色，是一种炭灰色，内部是较浅的灰色。这是一种实用的颜色，中性且容易隐藏污垢和磨损。底部的金属脚使其保持直立。最大的失望是它由人造革制成，而非广告所说的皮革。但如果你重视功能而非时尚，并且能以好价格买到，它是一个结实、制作精良的工作包。

**解释**：产品：大号水桶包｜声音：未提及｜其他：设计用于服务而非风格；线条不够优雅；有充足空间和隔层；拉链顺滑；肩带可调节且舒适；颜色为石板色/炭灰色，内部浅灰色；底部有金属脚；材质为人造革而非广告所说的皮革；适合重视功能的人。

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`

## S2-009　G255

**原文**：If you are serious about doing aerial imagery, you need this set. DJI does an EXCEPTIONAL job of equipping their aircraft with the best cameras, with the best features you can get in a pro-consumer aircraft. I honestly believe that the Mavic Mini 3 Pro cannot be improved upon. In the 'under 250 gram' class, this is the best you can get.<br /><br />However, not even DJI can equip their aircraft cameras to get the highest quality, most striking, most enhanced images that true experts want. So, DJI has collaborated with a few quality lens filter makers to give us what we want.<br /><br />Skyreat is one of those companies. I have their lens filter sets for all of my DJI aircraft. I get stunning images, every time. It takes some training to understand which environments and which desired results lead to which filter to use. But, as any professional imagery artist will tell, you need to know how to use your tools, to get the product you want.<br /><br />Skyreat has done an excellent job (as usual) with this set. Every filter is perfectly made and does exactly what you expect it to do. They fit in place of the stock lens ring without fuss. The light weight and super sturdy aircraft grade aluminum frames are made to exacting specs. Every lens is crystal clear, no flubs, no distortion, with exact perfection over the entire image.<br /><br />The carrying case is well made, protecting all the filters and fits easily into the aftermarket carrying case that I use for the aircraft. Overall, I am very pleased with this set of Skyreat lens filters. Good purchase.

**翻译**：如果你认真对待航拍影像，你需要这套装备。DJI在为其飞行器配备最佳相机方面做得非常出色，提供了专业消费级飞行器所能拥有的最佳功能。我真诚地相信，Mavic Mini 3 Pro无法再改进了。在‘250克以下’级别中，这是你能得到的最好的。<br /><br />然而，即使是DJI也无法为其飞行器相机配备能够获得真正专家所想要的最高质量、最引人注目、最增强的图像。因此，DJI与几家优质镜头滤镜制造商合作，为我们提供我们想要的东西。<br /><br />Skyreat就是其中一家公司。我有他们为所有DJI飞行器准备的镜头滤镜套装。每次都能获得令人惊叹的图像。需要一些训练来理解哪些环境和哪些期望结果导致使用哪种滤镜。但是，正如任何专业影像艺术家会告诉你的，你需要知道如何使用你的工具，才能获得你想要的产品。<br /><br />Skyreat在这套产品上做得非常出色（一如既往）。每个滤镜都制作完美，完全符合你的期望。它们可以轻松替换原装镜头环。轻量且超坚固的航空级铝制框架按照精确规格制造。每个镜头都晶莹剔透，没有瑕疵，没有失真，整个图像完美无缺。<br /><br />携带盒制作精良，保护所有滤镜，并且轻松放入我用于飞行器的售后携带盒中。总体而言，我对这套Skyreat镜头滤镜非常满意。购买得好。

**解释**：产品：Skyreat镜头滤镜套装｜声音：未提及声音｜其他：讨论航拍影像、DJI Mavic Mini 3 Pro、镜头滤镜、图像质量、制造质量、携带盒

**判定（决策方填）**：`___`
